B.3
hold. Then, for any
g,h\in[K]
,

\displaystyle\frac{\langle\widehat{M}_{g}-\widehat{M}_{h},Q\rangle-\langle M_{g}-M_{h},Q\rangle}{\sqrt{\big(\widehat{\sigma}_{g}^{2}\widehat{S}_{g}^{2}+\widehat{\sigma}_{h}^{2}\widehat{S}_{h}^{2}\big)d_{1}d_{2}/T^{1-\gamma}}}\longrightarrow N(0,1),

as
d_{1},d_{2},T\to\infty
.

The proof of Theorem
B.1
,
B.4
and Corollary
B.1
is almost the same with Theorem
1
,
4
and Corollary
1
. We provide the proof of Theorem
B.2
and
B.3
in Appendix
D.11
and
D.12
.

B.2
Efficient SVD implementation

Although Algorithm
1
enables straightforward theoretical analysis of convergence, it is computationally intensive due to the necessity of performing SVD on
d_{1}\times d_{2}
matrices at each time step. To address this, we introduce an alternative algorithm that offers greater computational efficiency for practical implementation.

Algorithm 3

\varepsilon
-greedy two-arm MCB with online gradient descent (fast SVD)

Input
: exploration probabilities
\{\varepsilon_{t}\}_{t\geq 1}
; step sizes
\{\eta_{t}\}_{t\geq 1}
; initializations with balanced factorization
{\widehat{M}}_{0,0}=\widehat{U}_{0,0}\widehat{V}_{0,0}^{\top}
,
{\widehat{M}}_{1,0}=\widehat{U}_{1,0}\widehat{V}_{1,0}^{\top}
.

Output
:
\widehat{M}_{0,T}
,
\widehat{M}_{1,T}
.

for

t=1,2,\cdots,T

do

Observe a new request
X_{t}
;

Calculate
\pi_{t}=(1-\varepsilon_{t})\mathbbm{1}\big(\langle{\widehat{M}}_{1,t-1}-{\widehat{M}}_{0,t-1},X_{t}\rangle>0\big)+\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{2}
;

Sample an action
a_{t}\sim\text{Bernoulli}(\pi_{t})
and get a reward
r_{t}
;

for

i=0,1

do

R_{U}D_{U}R_{U}^{\top}\leftarrow\text{SVD}(\widehat{U}_{i,t-1}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{i,t-1})
,

R_{V}D_{V}R_{V}^{\top}\leftarrow\text{SVD}(\widehat{V}_{i,t-1}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{i,t-1})
,

Q_{U}DQ_{V}\leftarrow\text{SVD}(D_{U}^{1/2}R_{U}^{\top}R_{V}D_{V}^{1/2})
,

Update by

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\widehat{U}_{i,t}\\
\widehat{V}_{i,t}\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}\widehat{U}_{i,t-1}\\
\widehat{V}_{i,t-1}\end{pmatrix}-

\displaystyle\eta_{t}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=i)}{i\pi_{t}+(1-i)(1-\pi_{t})}\cdot

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\big(\langle\widehat{U}_{i,t-1}\widehat{V}_{i,t-1}^{\top},X_{t}\rangle-r_{t}\big)X_{t}\widehat{V}_{a,t-1}R_{V}D_{V}^{-1/2}Q_{V}Q_{U}^{\top}D_{U}^{1/2}R_{U}^{\top}\\
\big(\langle\widehat{U}_{i,t-1}\widehat{V}_{i,t-1}^{\top},X_{t}\rangle-r_{t})X_{t}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{a,t-1}R_{U}D_{U}^{-1/2}Q_{U}Q_{V}^{\top}D_{V}^{1/2}R_{V}^{\top}\end{pmatrix},

Set
\widehat{M}_{i,t}=\widehat{U}_{i,t}\widehat{V}_{i,t}^{\top}
.

end

for

end

for

Algorithm
3
can be shown equivalent to Algorithm
1
in the following sense:

Lemma B.1

(
Jin et al. [2016]
Lemma 3.2)
.

Given the same observations and algorithmic parameters, the outputs of every time step
t
in Algorithm
1
, i.e.,
\widetilde{U}_{i,t}
,
\widetilde{V}_{i,t}
for
i=0,1
, and those for Algorithm
3
, i.e.,
\widehat{U}_{i,t}
and
\widehat{V}_{i,t}
, satisfy
\widetilde{U}_{i,t}\widetilde{V}_{i,t}^{\top}=\widehat{U}_{i,t}\widehat{V}_{i,t}^{\top}
.

Algorithm
3
only requires computing the SVD of the
r\times r
matrices
\widehat{U}_{i,t-1}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{i,t-1}
and
\widehat{V}_{i,t-1}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{i,t-1}
. This reduces the computational complexity of each SVD step from
O(d_{1}d_{2}^{2})
to
O(r^{3})
, and the computational complexity for matrix multiplication step is
O(d_{1}r^{2})
. Since the rank
r
is significantly smaller than the dimensions
d_{1}
and
d_{2}
and is typically a constant in many applications, the computational complexity remains manageable even as the dimensions scale rapidly. Consequently, the computational burden remains low in high-dimensional settings.

B.3
Non-uniform sampling

Our previous results assume that the covariates
X_{t}
are uniformly sampled from the orthonormal basis
\mathcal{X}=\{e_{j_{1}}e_{j_{2}}^{\top}:j_{1}\in[d_{1}],j_{2}\in[d_{2}]\}
, ensuring equal opportunity to make decisions for each product or block at every week or hour. While this assumption facilitates theoretical analysis, it may not hold in certain applications. For instance, in online recommendation systems, some products may be infrequently searched or visited by users, violating uniform sampling. To address this, we consider covariates sampled with known probabilities
p_{X}
for each
X\in\mathcal{X}
. Our methods remain applicable, albeit with more complex formulations for theoretical analysis.

For policy learning, all learning strategies remain the same except that the stochastic gradient descent step in Algorithm
1
should be modified to:

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\widetilde{U}_{1,t}\\
\widetilde{V}_{1,t}\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}\widehat{U}_{1,t-1}\\
\widehat{V}_{1,t-1}\end{pmatrix}-\frac{\eta_{t}}{\pi_{t}p_{X_{t}}}\cdot\begin{pmatrix}\big(\langle\widehat{U}_{1,t-1}\widehat{V}_{1,t-1}^{\top},X_{t}\rangle-r_{t}\big)X_{t}\widehat{V}_{1,t-1}\\
\big(\langle\widehat{U}_{1,t-1}\widehat{V}_{1,t-1}^{\top},X_{t}\rangle-r_{t})X_{t}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{1,t-1}\end{pmatrix}

and

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\widetilde{U}_{0,t}\\
\widetilde{V}_{0,t}\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}\widehat{U}_{0,t-1}\\
\widehat{V}_{0,t-1}\end{pmatrix}-\frac{\eta_{t}}{(1-\pi_{t})p_{X_{t}}}\cdot\begin{pmatrix}\big(\langle\widehat{U}_{0,t-1}\widehat{V}_{0,t-1}^{\top},X_{t}\rangle-r_{t}\big)X_{t}\widehat{V}_{0,t-1}\\
\big(\langle\widehat{U}_{0,t-1}\widehat{V}_{0,t-1}^{\top},X_{t}\rangle-r_{t})X_{t}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{0,t-1}\end{pmatrix}.

Define
p_{L}=\min_{X\in\mathcal{X}}p_{X}
, and set
T_{0}=C_{0}T^{1-\gamma}
for large
C_{0}
. When
t\leq T_{0}
, set a constant exploration probability
\varepsilon_{t}=\varepsilon
and the step size
\eta_{t}:=\eta=\frac{c_{1}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}\lambda_{\max}}
for some constant
c_{1}
; when
T_{0}<t\leq T
, set
\varepsilon_{t}\asymp t^{-\gamma}
and
\eta_{t}=\varepsilon_{t}\eta
. Assume that the sample size and SNR satisfy
T^{1-\gamma}\gg r\log(d_{1})/(p_{L}d_{2})
and
\lambda_{\min}^{2}/(\sigma_{0}^{2}+\sigma_{1}^{2})\gg r\log(d_{1})/(d_{2}p_{L}^{2}T^{1-\gamma})
. Under these settings, we can achieve the following convergence results for the online estimators:

\displaystyle\big\|\widehat{M}_{i,t}-M_{i}\big\|_{\rm F}^{2}\leq C_{3}\|\widehat{M}_{i,0}-M_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\left(1-\frac{c_{1}\log d_{1}}{4\kappa T^{1-\gamma}}\right)^{t}+C_{4}t\cdot\sigma_{i}^{2}\frac{rd_{1}\log^{4}d_{1}}{p_{L}T^{2-2\gamma}},\quad\forall t\leq T_{0};

\displaystyle\big\|\widehat{M}_{i,t}-M_{i}\big\|_{\rm F}^{2}\leq C_{5}\sigma_{i}^{2}\frac{rd_{1}\log^{4}d_{1}}{p_{L}T^{1-\gamma}},\quad\forall t>T_{0}.

For policy inference, the debiasing procedure would require slight modification:

\displaystyle\widehat{M}_{0}^{\textsf{IPW}}:

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T-T_{0}}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\widehat{M}_{0,t-1}+\frac{1}{T-T_{0}}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=0)}{(1-\pi_{t})p_{X_{t}}}\big(r_{t}-\langle\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X_{t}\rangle\big)X_{t}

\displaystyle\widehat{M}_{1}^{\textsf{IPW}}:

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T-T_{0}}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}+\frac{1}{T-T_{0}}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}p_{X_{t}}}\big(r_{t}-\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1},X_{t}\rangle\big)X_{t}.

Moreover, if the arm-optimality sets satisfy

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{X\in\Omega_{\emptyset}}1/p_{X}\langle X,{\mathcal{P}}_{M_{i}}(Q)\rangle^{2}}{\min\{\sum_{X\in\Omega_{1}}1/p_{X}\langle X,{\mathcal{P}}_{M_{i}}(Q)\rangle^{2},\sum_{X\in\Omega_{0}}1/p_{X}\langle X,{\mathcal{P}}_{M_{i}}(Q)\rangle^{2}\}}=o(1),\quad i\in\{0,1\},

then the following asymptotic normality holds:

\displaystyle\frac{\langle\widehat{M}_{0},Q\rangle-\langle M_{0},Q\rangle}{\sigma_{0}S_{0}\sqrt{1/T^{1-\gamma}}}\rightarrow N(0,1)\quad{\rm and}\quad\frac{\langle\widehat{M}_{1},Q\rangle-\langle M_{1},Q\rangle}{\sigma_{1}S_{1}\sqrt{1/T^{1-\gamma}}}\rightarrow N(0,1),

as
d_{1},d_{2},T\to\infty
, where

\displaystyle S_{1}^{2}=T^{-\gamma}\sum_{X\in\Omega_{1}}1/p_{X}\langle X,{\mathcal{P}}_{M_{1}}(Q)\rangle^{2}+C_{\gamma}\sum_{X\in\Omega_{0}}1/p_{X}\langle X,{\mathcal{P}}_{M_{1}}(Q)\rangle^{2}

\displaystyle S_{0}^{2}=T^{-\gamma}\sum_{X\in\Omega_{0}}1/p_{X}\langle X,{\mathcal{P}}_{M_{0}}(Q)\rangle^{2}+C_{\gamma}\sum_{X\in\Omega_{1}}1/p_{X}\langle X,{\mathcal{P}}_{M_{0}}(Q)\rangle^{2}.

B.4
Other bandit algorithms

We discuss two common bandit algorithms: upper confidence bound (UCB) (
Li et al. [2010]
) and Thompson sampling (TS) (
Agrawal and Goyal [2013]
) for matrix completion bandit.
Although both algorithms are well-established in the broader bandit literature, their applications to high-dimensional linear bandits remain relatively limited, let alone matrix completion bandits.

(1) Upper confidence bound (UCB)

The UCB algorithm effectively balances the trade-off between exploration and exploitation through the principle of
optimism in the face of uncertainty
. The fundamental idea behind UCB is to maintain an optimistic estimate of each arm’s potential reward, thereby encouraging the selection of arms that may yield higher rewards based on both observed data and inherent uncertainty. The uncertainty of estimated bandit parameters can be quantified by the sup-norm convergence rate. Therefore, at time
t
, we define

\displaystyle\widehat{p}_{i,t}:=C_{0}\widehat{\sigma}_{i}\sqrt{\frac{rd_{1}\log^{4}d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}},\quad\forall i\in\{0,1\}\ {\rm and}\ t\geq T_{0},

where
C_{0}>0
is a large constant. Then the action
a_{t}
is selected by

\displaystyle a_{t}:=\mathbbm{1}\left(\langle X_{t},\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}\rangle+\widehat{p}_{1,t}>\langle X_{t},\widehat{M}_{0,t-1}\rangle+\widehat{p}_{0,t}\right),\quad\forall\ t\geq T_{0}.

For simplicity, we still adopt a constant exploration probability during phase I when
t<T_{0}:\asymp T^{1-\gamma}
for some
\gamma\in(0,1)
. We can show that UCB algorithm achieves a regret of
\widetilde{O}\big(\bar{m}\cdot T^{1-\gamma}+\bar{\sigma}\cdot T^{(1+\gamma)/2}\sqrt{rd_{1}}\log^{2}d_{1}\big)
. However, policy inference under UCB algorithm is significantly challenging because of the inherent bias in
\widehat{M}_{i,t}
’s and that the action
a_{t}
is deterministic conditioned on
{\mathcal{F}}_{t-1}
and
X_{t}
. In contrast, the
\varepsilon
-greedy algorithm randomizes the action
a_{t}
and facilitates the IPW-based debiaing technique.

(2) Thompson sampling (TS)

Unlike UCB which takes a deterministic approach to balancing exploration and exploitation, Thompson sampling is a probabilistic strategy that uses Bayesian principles to guide decision-making. It works by maintaining a posterior distribution for the reward of each arm, which represents the uncertainty about the true reward.

We follow the Bayesian matrix completion method in
Alquier et al. [2014]
. Denote the
h
-th row of
U_{i}
as
U_{i}^{(h)\top}
, the
g
-th row of
V_{i}
as
V_{i}^{(g)\top}
, and the
l
-th column of
U_{i}
and
V_{i}
as
u_{i,l}
and
v_{i,l}
. Given some constant vector
\delta=(\delta_{1},\cdots,\delta_{r})
, we assign the Gaussian priors for
u_{i,l}
and
v_{i,l}
as
u_{i,l}\sim N(0,\delta_{l}I_{d_{1}})
and
v_{i,l}\sim N(0,\delta_{l}I_{d_{2}})
.

At each iteration
t
, suppose the context is
X_{t}=e_{i_{t}}e_{j_{t}}^{\top}
. We first draw
U_{i,t}^{(i_{t})\top}
and
V_{i,t}^{(j_{t})\top}
from their posteriors at time
t-1
for
i=0,1
. The action
a_{t}
is selected by

\displaystyle a_{t}:=\mathbbm{1}\left(U_{1,t}^{(i_{t})\top}V_{1,t}^{(j_{t})}>U_{0,t}^{(i_{t})\top}V_{0,t}^{(j_{t})}\right).

Then we can update the posterior distribution of
U_{a_{t}}^{(i_{t})\top}
and
V_{a_{t}}^{(j_{t})\top}
at time
t
as

\displaystyle U_{a_{t}}^{(i_{t})\top}\big|\mathcal{F}_{t},\delta\sim N(\bar{U}_{a_{t}}^{(i_{t})\top},\mathcal{V}_{a_{t},i_{t}})\quad\quad V_{a_{t}}^{(j_{t})\top}\big|\mathcal{F}_{t},\delta\sim N(\bar{V}_{a_{t}}^{(j_{t})\top},\mathcal{W}_{a_{t},j_{t}}),

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}_{a_{t},i_{t}}^{-1}=\text{diag}(\delta)^{-1}+2\beta V_{a_{t},t}^{(j_{t})}V_{a_{t},t}^{(j_{t})\top},\quad\bar{U}_{a_{t}}^{(i_{t})\top}=2r_{t}\beta V_{a_{t},t}^{(j_{t})\top}\mathcal{V}_{a_{t},i_{t}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{a_{t},j_{t}}^{-1}=\text{diag}(\delta)^{-1}+2\beta U_{a_{t},t}^{(i_{t})}U_{a_{t},t}^{(i_{t})\top},\quad\bar{V}_{a_{t}}^{(j_{t})\top}=2r_{t}\beta U_{a_{t},t}^{(i_{t})\top}\mathcal{W}_{a_{t},j_{t}},

and
\beta
is a tuning parameter depending on
\sigma_{i}
.

Appendix C
Proofs of Main Results

C.1
Proof of Theorem
1

We first state the convergence result under general choices of step size
\eta_{t}
and
\varepsilon_{t}
and provide the proof for this Theorem.

Theorem C.1
.

Suppose that the horizon
T\leq d_{1}^{50}
and the initializations are incoherent, satisfying
\|\widehat{M}_{0,0}-M_{0}\|_{\rm F}+\|\widehat{M}_{1,0}-M_{1}\|_{\rm F}\leq c_{0}\lambda_{\min}
for some small constant
c_{0}>0
. Let
\widehat{M}_{0,t},\widehat{M}_{1,t},t\in[T]
denote the output of Algorithm
1
.
There exist absolute constants
C_{1},C_{2},C_{3},C_{4}>0
such that if, for any
t\in[T]
,

\displaystyle\min\bigg\{\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\sigma_{0}^{2}+\sigma_{1}^{2}},\ d_{1}d_{2}\log d_{1}\bigg\}\geq C_{1}\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{(\eta_{\tau}\lambda_{\max})^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\cdot\frac{r\log^{2}d_{1}}{d_{2}}\quad{\rm and}\quad\max_{\tau\in[t]}\ \frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\leq C_{2}\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}},

(11)

then with probability at least
1-8td^{-200}
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{M}_{i,t}-M_{i}\|_{\rm F}^{2}\leq C_{3}\|\widehat{M}_{i,0}-M_{i}\|_{\rm F}^{2}\cdot\prod_{\tau=1}^{t}\Big(1-\frac{\eta_{\tau}\lambda_{\min}}{4d_{1}d_{2}}\Big)+C_{4}\sigma_{i}^{2}\frac{r\log^{2}d_{1}}{d_{2}}\cdot\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{(\eta_{\tau}\lambda_{\max})^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}

\displaystyle\|\widehat{M}_{i,t}-M_{i}\|_{\max}^{2}\leq C_{3}\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}r^{3}}{d_{1}d_{2}}\prod_{\tau=1}^{t}\Big(1-\frac{\eta_{\tau}\lambda_{\min}}{4d_{1}d_{2}}\Big)+C_{4}\sigma_{i}^{2}\frac{r^{3}\log^{2}d_{1}}{d_{1}d_{2}^{2}}\cdot\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{(\eta_{\tau}\lambda_{\max})^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}},

for
i=0,1
.

Proof of Theorem
C.1
.

Define event
{\mathcal{E}}_{t}
for
1\leq t\leq T
as

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\bigg\{\forall i\in\{0,1\},\forall\tau<t:\|\widehat{M}_{i,\tau}-M_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{\tau}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\|\widehat{M}_{i,0}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{\tau}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}},

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\|\widehat{M}_{i,\tau}-M_{i}\|_{\max}^{2}\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{\tau}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}+\sum_{k=1}^{\tau}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}^{2}},

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\|e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{i,\tau}\widehat{V}_{i,\tau}^{\top}\|^{2}\leq\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}},\|e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{i,\tau}\widehat{U}_{i,\tau}^{\top}\|^{2}\leq\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}},\quad\forall l\in[d_{1}],j\in[d_{2}]\bigg\},

where
f(\eta_{k})=\frac{\eta_{k}\lambda_{\min}}{d_{1}d_{2}}
and
\mu_{0}=100\mu
.

Recall in Algorithm
1
, at each step, we update
\widetilde{U}_{i,t}
and
\widetilde{V}_{i,t}
by

\displaystyle\widetilde{U}_{i,t}

\displaystyle=\widehat{U}_{i,t-1}-\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=i)}{i\pi_{t}+(1-i)(1-\pi_{t})}\eta_{t}(\langle\widehat{U}_{i,t-1}\widehat{V}_{i,t-1}^{\top},X_{t}\rangle-r_{t})X_{t}\widehat{V}_{i,t-1},

\displaystyle\widetilde{V}_{i,t}

\displaystyle=\widehat{V}_{i,t-1}-\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=i)}{i\pi_{t}+(1-i)(1-\pi_{t})}\eta_{t}(\langle\widehat{U}_{i,t-1}\widehat{V}_{i,t-1}^{\top},X_{t}\rangle-r_{t})X_{t}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{i,t-1}.

Then

\displaystyle\widehat{U}_{i,t}\widehat{V}_{i,t}^{\top}=

\displaystyle\widetilde{U}_{i,t}\widetilde{V}_{i,t}^{\top}=\widehat{U}_{i,t-1}\widehat{V}_{i,t-1}^{\top}-\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=i)}{i\pi_{t}+(1-i)(1-\pi_{t})}\eta_{t}(\langle U_{i,t-1}V_{i,t-1}^{\top},X_{t}\rangle-y_{t})X_{t}\widehat{V}_{i,t-1}\widehat{V}_{i,t-1}^{\top}

\displaystyle-\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=i)}{i\pi_{t}+(1-i)(1-\pi_{t})}\eta_{t}(\langle\widehat{U}_{i,t-1}\widehat{V}_{i,t-1}^{\top},X_{t}\rangle-r_{t})\widehat{U}_{i,t-1}\widehat{U}_{i,t-1}^{\top}X_{t}

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=i)}{i\pi_{t}^{2}+(1-i)(1-\pi_{t})^{2}}\eta_{t}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{i,t-1}\widehat{V}_{i,t-1}^{\top},X_{t}\rangle-r_{t})^{2}X_{t}\widehat{V}_{i,t-1}\widehat{U}_{i,t-1}^{\top}X_{t}.

Without loss of generality, in the rest of the proof, we only consider
i=1
and omit the subscript
i
, then
i=0
case can be proved similarly. Let

\displaystyle\Delta_{t}=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\eta_{t}(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})X_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\eta_{t}(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}X_{t}

\displaystyle-\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\eta_{t}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})^{2}X_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}X_{t},

where
\pi_{t}={\mathbb{P}}(a_{t}=1|X_{t},\mathcal{F}_{t-1})
. In the following steps, we prove that if event
\mathcal{E}_{t-1}
happens, event
\mathcal{E}_{t}
will happen with high probability.

Step 1: bounding
\|\widehat{U}_{t}\widehat{V}_{t}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
.

We have
\widehat{U}_{t}\widehat{V}_{t}=\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-\Delta_{t}
and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\|\widehat{U}_{t}\widehat{V}_{t}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]=\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\langle{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}],\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\rangle+{\mathbb{E}}[\|\Delta_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}].

We first compute
2\langle{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}],\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\rangle
,

\displaystyle 2\langle{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}],\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\rangle=\frac{2\eta_{t}}{d_{1}d_{2}}\big(\|(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\widehat{V}_{t-1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\quad-2\eta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\left(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle^{2}\langle X_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}X_{t},\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\rangle\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad+\xi_{t}^{2}\langle X_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}X_{t},\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\rangle\right)|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta_{t}\lambda_{\min}}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{4\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}}\|\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}\|_{\max}\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}(\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}).

It is clear to see that
\|(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\widehat{V}_{t-1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\geq\frac{\lambda_{\min}}{2}\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
, and we apply
\frac{1}{\pi_{t}}\leq\frac{2}{\varepsilon_{t}}
in the latter term. Applying the incoherence property of
\widehat{U}_{t-1},\widehat{V}_{t-1}
,
U
and
V
and Cauchy Schwarz inequality,
\|\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}\|_{\max}=O(\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}})
and
\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}\leq\frac{2\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}}
under event
\mathcal{E}_{t-1}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle 2\langle{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}],\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta_{t}\lambda_{\min}}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{8\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}(\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sigma^{2}).

Then we compute
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\Delta_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]
. Since
(a+b+c)^{2}\leq 3(a^{2}+b^{2}+c^{2})
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\Delta_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\leq 3{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\eta_{t}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})^{2}\|X_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+3{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\eta_{t}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})^{2}\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}X_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+3{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{4}}\eta_{t}^{4}(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})^{4}\|X_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}X_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right].

For the first expectation, we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\eta_{t}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})^{2}\|X_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left[\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\|\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\|_{2,\max}^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}[\xi_{t}^{2}]{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|X_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}}\left(\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}+\sigma^{2}\frac{1}{d_{2}}\|\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}+\frac{2\eta_{t}^{2}r\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{2}}O(\lambda_{\max}^{2}).

The last inequality holds since
\|\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq r\|\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\|^{2}
and
\|\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\|^{2}=O(\lambda_{\max}^{2})
.

By symmetry,
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\eta_{t}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})^{2}\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}X_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\leq\frac{2\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}+\frac{2\eta_{t}^{2}r\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}}O(\lambda_{\max}^{2})
. Similarly, the last expectation can be written as

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{4}}\eta_{t}^{4}(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})^{4}\|X_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}X_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\eta_{t}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{t}^{3}}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left[\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle^{4}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\|\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}\|_{\max}^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}(\xi_{t}^{4})\|\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}\|_{\max}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{t}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{t}^{3}}\left(\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}\sum(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)_{ij}^{2}\right)\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}+\frac{\eta_{t}^{4}\sigma^{4}}{\varepsilon_{t}^{3}}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{t}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{t}^{3}}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{1}^{3}d_{2}^{3}}\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{t}^{4}\sigma^{4}}{\varepsilon_{t}^{3}}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}.

As long as
\eta_{t}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}^{1/2}}{\sqrt{\mu_{0}r}\lambda_{\max}}
and
\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\gg\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}^{2}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}r\kappa^{2}}
, the second order terms of
\eta_{t}
above will dominate the fourth order terms. Above all, for some positive constants
c_{1}
and
c_{2}
,

\displaystyle\quad-2{\mathbb{E}}\left(\langle\Delta_{t},\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\rangle|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right)+{\mathbb{E}}\left(\|\Delta_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right)

\displaystyle\leq(-\frac{\eta_{t}\lambda_{\min}}{d_{1}d_{2}}+\frac{c_{1}\eta_{t}^{2}\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}^{2}})\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{c_{2}\eta_{t}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq-f(\eta_{t})\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{c_{2}\eta_{t}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{2}},

where
f(\eta_{t})=\frac{\eta_{t}\lambda_{\min}}{2d_{1}d_{2}}
. The second order term
\frac{c_{1}\eta_{t}^{2}\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}^{2}}
is dominated as long as
\eta_{t}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{t}d_{2}}{\mu_{0}r\kappa\lambda_{\max}}
.

Next, we use telescoping to derive the relationship between
\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
and
\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
. Notice that,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{U}_{t}\widehat{V}_{t}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-\Delta_{t}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\langle\Delta_{t},\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\rangle+\|\Delta_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2(\langle\Delta_{t},\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\rangle-{\mathbb{E}}(\langle\Delta_{t},\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\rangle|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}))

\displaystyle\quad+(\|\Delta_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}(\|\Delta_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}))-2{\mathbb{E}}(\langle\Delta_{t},\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\rangle|\mathcal{F}_{t-1})+{\mathbb{E}}(\|\Delta_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1})

\displaystyle\leq(1-f(\eta_{t}))\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{c_{2}\eta_{t}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{2({\mathbb{E}}(\langle\Delta_{t},\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\rangle|\mathcal{F}_{t-1})-\langle\Delta_{t},\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\rangle)}_{A_{t}}+\underbrace{(\|\Delta_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}(\|\Delta_{t}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}))}_{B_{t}}

\displaystyle=(1-f(\eta_{t}))(1-f(\eta_{t-1}))\|\widehat{U}_{t-2}\widehat{V}_{t-2}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{c_{2}\eta_{t}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{2}}+(1-f(\eta_{t}))\frac{c_{2}\eta_{t-1}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t-1}d_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+(1-f(\eta_{t}))(A_{t-1}+B_{t-1})+(A_{t}+B_{t})

\displaystyle=\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))\frac{c_{2}\eta_{\tau}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{2}}+\frac{c_{2}\eta_{t}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))A_{\tau}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))B_{\tau}+A_{t}+B_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{c_{2}\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))A_{\tau}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))B_{\tau}+A_{t}+B_{t}.

In Lemma
1
, we show
\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))A_{\tau}+A_{t}
and
\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))B_{\tau}+B_{t}
will be dominated.

Lemma 1
.

For any
1\leq t\leq T
, under the conditions in Theorem
1
and event
\mathcal{E}_{t-1}
, with probability at least
1-td_{1}^{-200}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))A_{\tau}+A_{t}\lesssim\gamma\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\gamma\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}},

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))B_{\tau}+B_{t}\lesssim\gamma\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\gamma\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}}.

Combine with the union bound, with probability at least
1-td_{1}^{-200}
,

\displaystyle\|U_{t}V_{t}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\|U_{0}V_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}}.

Step 2: proof of incoherence.

In Lemma
2
, we prove the incoherence property holds true for every
U_{t}
and
V_{t}
.

Lemma 2
.

For any
1\leq t\leq T
, under the conditions in Theorem
1
and event
\mathcal{E}_{t-1}
, with probability at least
1-td_{1}^{-200}
,

\displaystyle\|e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{i,\tau}\widehat{V}_{i,\tau}^{\top}\|^{2}\leq\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}\quad\|e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{i,\tau}\widehat{U}_{i,\tau}^{\top}\|^{2}\leq\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}},\quad\forall l\in[d_{1}],j\in[d_{2}],

where
\mu_{0}=100\mu
.

Step 3: bounding
\|\widehat{U}_{t}\widehat{V}_{t}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}
.

In order to bound
\|\widehat{U}_{t}\widehat{V}_{t}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}
, we need to bound
\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t}\widehat{V}_{t}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}
and
\|e_{j}^{\top}(\widehat{V}_{t}\widehat{U}_{t}^{\top}-M^{\top})\|^{2}
for any
l\in[d_{1}],j\in[d_{2}]
first.

Lemma 3
.

For any
1\leq t\leq T
, under the conditions in Theorem
1
and event
\mathcal{E}_{t-1}
, with probability at least
1-d_{1}^{-200}
,

\displaystyle\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t}\widehat{V}_{t}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{d_{1}}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}},

\displaystyle\|e_{j}^{\top}(\widehat{V}_{t}\widehat{U}_{t}^{\top}-M^{\top})\|^{2}\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{d_{2}}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}^{2}}

for
\forall l\in[d_{1}],j\in[d_{2}]
.

Now we are ready to prove the upper bound for
\|\widehat{U}_{t}\widehat{V}_{t}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}
. For simplicity, denote
\widehat{M}_{t}=\widehat{U}_{t}\widehat{V}_{t}^{\top}
.The SVD of
\widehat{M}_{t}
and
M
are
M_{t}=\widehat{L}_{t}\widehat{\Lambda}_{t}\widehat{R}_{t}
and
M=L\Lambda R
, respectively. Then,
\widehat{M}_{t}-M
can be written as

\displaystyle\widehat{M}_{t}-M

\displaystyle=\widehat{L}_{t}\widehat{L}_{t}^{\top}\widehat{M}_{t}\widehat{R}_{t}\widehat{R}_{t}^{\top}-LL^{\top}MRR^{\top}

\displaystyle=\widehat{L}_{t}\widehat{L}_{t}^{\top}(\widehat{M}_{t}-M)\widehat{R}_{t}\widehat{R}_{t}^{\top}+(\widehat{L}_{t}\widehat{L}_{t}^{\top}-LL^{\top})M\widehat{R}_{t}\widehat{R}_{t}^{\top}+LL^{\top}M(\widehat{R}_{t}\widehat{R}_{t}^{\top}-RR^{\top})

As a result, for any
l\in[d_{1}]
,
j\in[d_{2}]
,
e_{l}^{\top}(M_{t}-M)e_{j}
can be decomposed as

\displaystyle\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{M}_{t}-M)e_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\underbrace{\|e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{L}_{t}\widehat{L}_{t}^{\top}(\widehat{M}_{t}-M)\widehat{R}_{t}\widehat{R}_{t}^{\top}e_{j}\|^{2}}_{I}+\underbrace{\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{L}_{t}\widehat{L}_{t}^{\top}-LL^{\top})M\widehat{R}_{t}\widehat{R}_{t}^{\top}e_{j}\|^{2}}_{II}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\|e_{l}^{\top}LL^{\top}M(\widehat{R}_{t}\widehat{R}_{t}^{\top}-RR^{\top})e_{j}\|^{2}}_{III}.

Term
I
can be bounded in the following way,

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle\leq\|e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{L}_{t}\widehat{L}_{t}^{\top}\|^{2}\|M_{t}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\|\widehat{R}_{t}\widehat{R}_{t}^{\top}e_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}r}{d_{1}}\left(\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\|\widehat{M}_{0}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}}\right)\frac{\mu_{0}r}{d_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\|\widehat{M}_{0}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}^{2}}.

The last inequality holds since
\|\widehat{M}_{0}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lesssim\lambda_{\min}^{2}
. Next, we look at Term
II
. Let
O_{\widehat{L}_{t}}=\mathop{\arg\min}_{O\in\mathbb{O}_{r}}\|\widehat{L}_{t}-LO\|
, where
\mathbb{O}_{r}
denotes the set of all
r\times r
real matrices with orthonormal columns, then

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{L}_{t}-LO_{\widehat{L}_{t}})\widehat{L}_{t}^{\top}M\widehat{R}_{t}\widehat{R}_{t}^{\top}e_{j}\|^{2}+\|e_{l}^{\top}LO_{\widehat{L}_{t}}(\widehat{L}_{t}^{\top}-O_{\widehat{L}_{t}}^{\top}L^{\top})M\widehat{R}_{t}\widehat{R}_{t}^{\top}e_{j}\|^{2}.

The second part can be bounded by,

\displaystyle\|e_{l}^{\top}LO_{\widehat{L}_{t}}(\widehat{L}_{t}^{\top}-O_{\widehat{L}_{t}}^{\top}L^{\top})M\widehat{R}_{t}\widehat{R}_{t}^{\top}e_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|e_{l}^{\top}LO_{\widehat{L}_{t}}\|^{2}\|\widehat{L}_{t}^{\top}-O_{\widehat{L}_{t}}^{\top}L^{\top}\|^{2}\|M\widehat{R}_{t}\widehat{R}_{t}^{\top}e_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu r}{d_{1}}\|\widehat{L}_{t}^{\top}-O_{\widehat{L}_{t}}^{\top}L^{\top}\|^{2}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}},

where we use the incoherence property and the consistency of matrix norm and vector norm. By Wedin’s
\sin\Theta
Theorem (
Davis and Kahan [1970]
,
Wedin [1972]
),

\displaystyle\|\widehat{L}_{t}^{\top}-O_{\widehat{L}_{t}}^{\top}L^{\top}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\|\widehat{M}_{t}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\|\widehat{M}_{0}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}r\kappa^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\gamma\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})+\gamma\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}r\kappa^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}}.

The first part can be bounded by the upper bound of
\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t}\widehat{V}_{t}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}
we have obtained in Lemma
3
,

\displaystyle\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{L}_{t}-LO_{\widehat{L}_{t}})L_{t}^{\top}M\widehat{R}_{t}\widehat{R}_{t}^{\top}e_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|e_{i}^{\top}(\widehat{L}_{t}-LO_{\widehat{L}_{t}})\|^{2}\|\widehat{L}_{t}^{\top}M\|^{2}\|\widehat{R}_{t}\widehat{R}_{t}^{\top}e_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|e_{i}^{\top}(\widehat{L}_{t}-LO_{\widehat{L}_{t}})\|^{2}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}.

By Theorem3.7 in
Cape et al. [2019]
and Wedin’s
\sin\Theta
Theorem again,

\displaystyle\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{L}_{t}-LO_{\widehat{L}_{t}})\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\frac{\|(I-LL^{\top})(\widehat{M}_{t}-M)\|_{2,\max}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\|\sin\Theta(\widehat{L}_{t},L)\|^{2}\|L\|_{2,\max}

\displaystyle\leq 4\frac{\|\widehat{M}_{t}-M\|_{2,\max}+\|LL^{\top}\|\|\widehat{M}_{t}-M\|_{2,\max}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\|\sin\Theta(\widehat{L}_{t},L)\|^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d_{1}}}

\displaystyle\leq 8\frac{\|\widehat{M}_{t}-M\|_{2,\max}}{\lambda_{\min}}+2\frac{\|\widehat{M}_{t}-M\|_{\rm{F}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d_{1}}}.

Then

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}(\widehat{L}_{t}-LO_{\widehat{L}_{t}})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\widehat{M}_{t}-M\|_{2,\max}^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}+\frac{\|\widehat{M}_{t}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\frac{\mu r}{d_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\prod_{k=t}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)}{d_{1}}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}}.

Above all,

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle\lesssim\prod_{k=t}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}^{2}}.

By symmetry, following the same arguments, III will also have the same upper bound.

Combine the above three terms together as well as the union bound, we can conclude that with probability at least
1-8td_{1}^{-200}
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{M}_{t}-M\|_{\max}^{2}\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}^{2}}.

Next we derive the upper bound of
\|\widehat{M}_{i,t}-M_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
under specific choices of
\eta_{t}
and
\varepsilon_{t}
for all
1\leq t\leq T
, and the entrywise error bound can be derived following the same arguments. When
1\leq t\leq T_{0}=C_{0}T^{1-\gamma}
,
\eta_{t}=\eta=\frac{c_{1}d_{1}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}
and
\varepsilon_{t}=\varepsilon
. Then

\displaystyle\|\widehat{M}_{i,t}-M_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{M}_{i,0}-M_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\prod_{\tau=1}^{t}(1-\frac{c_{1}\log d_{1}}{4\kappa T^{1-\gamma}})+\sigma_{i}^{2}\frac{r\log^{2}d_{1}}{d_{2}}\frac{tc_{1}^{2}d_{1}^{2}d_{2}^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{T^{2(1-\gamma)}\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{M}_{i,0}-M_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}(1-\frac{c_{1}\log d_{1}}{4\kappa T^{1-\gamma}})^{t}+\frac{trd_{1}^{2}d_{2}\log^{4}d_{1}}{T^{2(1-\gamma)}}.

When
T_{0}+1\leq t\leq T
,
\varepsilon_{t}=c_{2}t^{-\gamma}
and
\eta_{t}=\varepsilon_{t}\eta
. Note that the contraction term becomes
(1-\frac{c_{1}\log d_{1}}{4\kappa T^{1-\gamma}})^{T_{0}}\prod_{\tau=T_{0}+1}^{t}(1-\frac{c_{1}c_{2}\varepsilon_{t}\log d_{1}}{4\kappa T^{1-\gamma}})\leq(1-\frac{c_{1}\log d_{1}}{4\kappa T^{1-\gamma}})^{C_{0}T^{1-\gamma}}=O(d_{1}^{-C_{0}c_{1}/4\kappa})
. Since
C_{0}
can be sufficiently large,
O(d_{1}^{-C_{0}c_{1}/4\kappa})
can be small enough to make the first computational error term dominated by the second statistical error term. Therefore,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{M}_{i,t}-M_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma_{i}^{2}rd_{1}^{2}d_{2}\log^{4}d_{1}\frac{T^{1-\gamma}+\sum_{\tau=T_{0}+1}^{t}\tau^{-\gamma}}{T^{2(1-\gamma)}}\lesssim\frac{\sigma_{i}^{2}rd_{1}^{2}d_{2}\log^{4}d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}},

(12)

where the second inequality comes from the fact that
\sum_{\tau=T_{0}+1}^{t}\tau^{-\gamma}\leq\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\tau^{-\gamma}=O(t^{1-\gamma})\leq O(T^{1-\gamma})
.
∎

C.2
Proof of Theorem
2

Proof.

Without loss of generality, assume at time
t
, arm 1 is optimal, i.e.,
\langle M_{1}-M_{0},X_{t}\rangle>0
, then the expected regret is

\displaystyle r_{t}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\langle M_{1}-M_{0},X_{t}\rangle\mathbbm{1}(\text{choose arm 0})\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\langle M_{1}-M_{0},X_{t}\rangle\mathbbm{1}(\text{choose arm 0},\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X_{t}\rangle\geq 0)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\langle M_{1}-M_{0},X_{t}\rangle\mathbbm{1}(\text{choose arm 0},\langle\widehat{M}_{0,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{1,t-1},X_{t}\rangle>0)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{2}\|M_{1}-M_{0}\|_{\max}+\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\langle M_{1}-M_{0},X_{t}\rangle\mathbbm{1}(\langle\widehat{M}_{0,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{1,t-1},X_{t}\rangle>0)\right]}_{(2)}.

Define
\delta_{t}^{2}
the entrywise bound in Theorem
1
such that
\|U_{i,t}V_{i,t}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}\leq\delta_{t}^{2}
with probability at least
1-8td_{1}^{-200}
, and event
B_{t}=\{\langle M_{1}-M_{0},X_{t}\rangle>2\delta_{t}\}
, then the latter expectation can be written as

\displaystyle(2)

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\langle M_{1}-M_{0},X_{t}\rangle\mathbbm{1}\left(\langle\widehat{M}_{0,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{1,t-1},X_{t}\rangle>0\cap B_{t}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\langle M_{1}-M_{0},X_{t}\rangle\mathbbm{1}\left(\langle\widehat{M}_{0,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{1,t-1},X_{t}\rangle>0\cap B_{t}^{c}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\|M_{1}-M_{0}\|_{\max}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathbbm{1}\left(\langle\widehat{M}_{0,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{1,t-1},X_{t}\rangle>0\cap B_{t}\right)\right]+2\delta_{t}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathbbm{1}(B_{t}^{c})\right].

Under event
B_{t}
and
\langle\widehat{M}_{0,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{1,t-1},X_{t}\rangle>0
,

\displaystyle 0>\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X_{t}\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-M_{1},X_{t}\rangle+\langle M_{0}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X_{t}\rangle+\langle M_{1}-M_{0},X_{t}\rangle

\displaystyle>\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-M_{1},X_{t}\rangle+\langle M_{0}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X_{t}\rangle+2\delta_{t}.

This means either
\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-M_{1},X_{t}\rangle<-\delta_{t}
or
\langle M_{0}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X_{t}\rangle<-\delta_{t}
, which further implies either
\|\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-M_{1}\|_{\max}
or
\|M_{0}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1}\|_{\max}
should be larger than
\delta_{t}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathbbm{1}(\langle{M}_{0,t-1}-{M}_{1,t-1},X_{t}\rangle>0)\cap B_{t}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{P}}\left(\langle{M}_{0,t-1}-{M}_{1,t-1},X_{t}\rangle>0\cap B_{t}\right)

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{P}}\left(\|{M}_{1,t-1}-M_{1}\|_{\max}>\delta_{t}\right)+{\mathbb{P}}\left(\|{M}_{0,t-1}-M_{0}\|_{\max}>\delta_{t}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16(t-1)}{d^{200}},

For the other term, since
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathbbm{1}(B_{t}^{c})\right]={\mathbb{P}}(\langle M_{1}-M_{0},X_{t}\rangle<2\delta_{t})\leq 1
, then

\displaystyle r_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon_{t}\|M_{1}-M_{0}\|_{\max}+\frac{16(t-1)}{d^{200}}\|M_{1}-M_{0}\|_{\max}+2\delta_{t}.

The total regret up to time
T
is

\displaystyle R_{T}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}r_{t}\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\varepsilon_{t}\|M_{1}-M_{0}\|_{\max}+\frac{8T(T-1)}{d_{1}^{200}}\|M_{1}-M_{0}\|_{\max}+2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\delta_{t}.

In Theorem
1
, we have shown that
\delta_{t}^{2}=C_{3}\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}r^{3}}{d_{1}d_{2}}\prod_{\tau=1}^{t}(1-\frac{c_{1}\log d_{1}}{4\kappa T^{1-\gamma}})+C_{4}\frac{t\cdot rd_{1}\log^{4}d_{1}\sigma_{i}^{2}}{T^{2(1-\gamma)}}
when
1\leq t\leq T_{0}
and
\delta_{t}^{2}=C_{5}\frac{rd_{1}\log^{4}d_{1}\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{T^{1-\gamma}}
when
T_{0}+1\leq t\leq T
, for some constants
C_{3}
,
C_{4}
and
C_{5}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle R_{T}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{t=1}^{T_{0}}\varepsilon\|M_{1}-M_{0}\|_{\max}+\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{1}{t^{\gamma}}\|M_{1}-M_{0}\|_{\max}+\frac{\|M_{1}-M_{0}\|_{\max}T^{2}}{d_{1}^{200}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{t=1}^{T_{0}}\sqrt{\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}r^{3}}{d_{1}d_{2}}\prod_{\tau=1}^{t}(1-\frac{c_{1}\log d_{1}}{4\kappa T^{1-\gamma}})}+\sum_{t=1}^{T_{0}}\sqrt{\frac{t\cdot rd_{1}\log^{4}d_{1}\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{T^{2(1-\gamma)}}}+\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{rd_{1}\log^{4}d_{1}\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}.

Since
T\ll d_{1}^{50}
, the third term is negligible. As a result, from the fact that
\sum_{t=1}^{T}t^{-\gamma}\lesssim T^{1-\gamma}
,

\displaystyle R_{T}

\displaystyle\lesssim T^{1-\gamma}\|M_{1}-M_{0}\|_{\max}+T^{1-\gamma}\frac{\lambda_{\min}r^{3/2}}{\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}}+T^{(1+\gamma)/2}\sqrt{rd_{1}\log^{4}d_{1}\bar{\sigma}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim T^{1-\gamma}(\|M_{1}\|_{\max}+\|M_{0}\|_{\max})+T^{(1+\gamma)/2}\sqrt{rd_{1}\log^{4}d_{1}\bar{\sigma}^{2}}.

∎

C.3
Proof of Theorem
3

Proof.

Without loss of generality, we only consider the proof for
i=1
, and omit all the subscript.

We follow the arguments in
Xia and Yuan [2021]
to decompose
\langle\widehat{M},Q\rangle-\langle M,Q\rangle
. Notice that

\displaystyle\langle\widehat{M},Q\rangle-\langle M,Q\rangle=\langle\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}\widehat{Z}\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top},Q\rangle+\langle\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}M\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top}-M,Q\rangle.

Let
b=\frac{T}{T-T_{0}}
, notice that
b=O(1)
and will converge to 1 as
T\rightarrow\infty
. We can write
\widehat{M}^{\textsf{IPW}}
in (
4
) into

\displaystyle\widehat{M}^{\textsf{IPW}}

\displaystyle=\frac{b}{T}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\widehat{M}_{t-1}+\frac{b}{T}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}(y_{t}-\langle{M}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle)X_{t}

\displaystyle=M_{1}+\underbrace{\frac{b}{T}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\xi_{t}X_{t}}_{=:\widehat{Z}_{1}}+\underbrace{\frac{b}{T}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\left(\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\langle\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle X_{t}-\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\right)}_{=:\widehat{Z}_{2}}.

Moreover, recall that
\widehat{L}
and
\widehat{R}
are the top-
r
left and right singular vectors of
\widehat{M}^{\textsf{IPW}}
. Define
(d_{1}+d_{2})\times(2r)
matrices

{\Theta}=\begin{pmatrix}L&0\\
0&R\end{pmatrix}\quad\widehat{\Theta}=\begin{pmatrix}\widehat{L}&0\\
0&\widehat{R}\end{pmatrix}\quad A=\begin{pmatrix}0&M\\
M^{\top}&0\end{pmatrix}.

Then, we can write

\widehat{\Theta}\widehat{\Theta}^{\top}A\widehat{\Theta}\widehat{\Theta}^{\top}-\Theta\Theta^{\top}A\Theta\Theta^{\top}=\begin{pmatrix}0&\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}M\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top}-M\\
(\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}M\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top}-M)^{\top}&0\end{pmatrix}.

We further define

\widetilde{Q}=\begin{pmatrix}0&Q\\
0&0\end{pmatrix}\quad\text{and}\quad\widehat{E}=\begin{pmatrix}0&\widehat{Z}\\
\widehat{Z}^{\top}&0\end{pmatrix},

where
\widehat{Z}=\widehat{Z}_{1}+\widehat{Z}_{2}
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\langle\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}M\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top}-M,Q\rangle=\langle\widehat{\Theta}\widehat{\Theta}^{\top}A\widehat{\Theta}\widehat{\Theta}^{\top}-\Theta\Theta^{\top}A\Theta\Theta^{\top},\widetilde{Q}\rangle.

Define

\displaystyle\mathfrak{P}^{-s}=\left\{\begin{aligned} \begin{pmatrix}L\Lambda^{-s}L^{\top}&0\\
0&R\Lambda^{-s}R^{\top}\end{pmatrix}\quad\text{if $s$ is even}\\
\begin{pmatrix}0&L\Lambda^{-s}R^{\top}\\
R\Lambda^{-s}L^{\top}&0\end{pmatrix}\quad\text{if $s$ is odd,}\end{aligned}\right.

and

\displaystyle\mathfrak{P}^{0}=\mathfrak{P}^{\perp}=\begin{pmatrix}L_{\perp}L_{\perp}^{\top}&0\\
0&R_{\perp}R_{\perp}^{\top}\end{pmatrix}.

By Theorem 1 in
Xia [2021]
,
\widehat{\Theta}\widehat{\Theta}^{\top}-\Theta\Theta^{\top}
has explicit representation formula of empirical spectral projector, in the form of

\displaystyle\widehat{\Theta}\widehat{\Theta}^{\top}-\Theta\Theta^{\top}=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\mathcal{S}_{A,k}(\widehat{E})

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{A,k}(\widehat{E})=\sum_{s_{1}+\cdots+s_{k+1}=k}^{k}(-1)^{1+\tau(s)}\mathfrak{P}^{-s_{1}}\widehat{E}\mathfrak{P}^{-s_{2}}\cdots\mathfrak{P}^{-s_{k}}\widehat{E}\mathfrak{P}_{-s_{k+1}}

where
s_{1},\cdots,s_{k+1}\geq 0
are integers,
\tau(\mathbf{s})=\sum_{i=1}^{k+1}\mathbbm{1}(s_{i}>0)
.

As a result,

\displaystyle\widehat{\Theta}\widehat{\Theta}^{\top}A\widehat{\Theta}\widehat{\Theta}^{\top}-\Theta\Theta^{\top}A\Theta\Theta^{\top}

\displaystyle=(\mathcal{S}_{A,1}(\widehat{E})A\Theta\Theta^{\top}+\Theta\Theta^{\top}A\mathcal{S}_{A,1}(\widehat{E}))+\sum_{k=2}^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{A,k}(\widehat{E})A\Theta\Theta^{\top}+\Theta\Theta^{\top}A\mathcal{S}_{A,k}(\widehat{E}))

\displaystyle\quad+(\widehat{\Theta}\widehat{\Theta}^{\top}-\Theta\Theta^{\top})A(\widehat{\Theta}\widehat{\Theta}^{\top}-\Theta\Theta^{\top}).

By definition of
\mathcal{S}_{A,1}(\widehat{E})
,

\displaystyle\langle\mathcal{S}_{A,1}(\widehat{E})A\Theta\Theta^{\top}+\Theta\Theta^{\top}A\mathcal{S}_{A,1}(\widehat{E}),\widetilde{Q}\rangle=\langle LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}R_{\perp}R_{\perp}^{\top},Q\rangle+\langle L_{\perp}L_{\perp}^{\top}\widehat{Z}RR^{\top},Q\rangle

\displaystyle\quad=\langle LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{1}R_{\perp}R_{\perp}^{\top},Q\rangle+\langle L_{\perp}L_{\perp}^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{1}RR^{\top},Q\rangle+\langle LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{2}R_{\perp}R_{\perp}^{\top},Q\rangle+\langle L_{\perp}L_{\perp}^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{2}RR^{\top},Q\rangle.

Combine all the terms above,
\langle\widehat{M},Q\rangle-\langle M,Q\rangle
can be decomposed into

\displaystyle\langle\widehat{M},Q\rangle-\langle M,Q\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{1}R_{\perp}R_{\perp}^{\top},Q\rangle+\langle L_{\perp}L_{\perp}^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{1}RR^{\top},Q\rangle+\langle LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{1}RR^{\top},Q\rangle

(13)

\displaystyle\quad+\langle LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{2}R_{\perp}R_{\perp}^{\top},Q\rangle+\langle L_{\perp}L_{\perp}^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{2}RR^{\top},Q\rangle+\langle LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{2}RR^{\top},Q\rangle

(14)

\displaystyle\quad+\langle\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}\widehat{Z}\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top},Q\rangle-\langle LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}RR^{\top},Q\rangle

(15)

\displaystyle\quad+\langle\sum_{k=2}^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{A,k}(\widehat{E})A\Theta\Theta^{\top}+\Theta\Theta^{\top}A\mathcal{S}_{A,k}(\widehat{E})),\widetilde{Q}\rangle+\langle(\widehat{\Theta}\widehat{\Theta}^{\top}-\Theta\Theta^{\top})A(\widehat{\Theta}\widehat{\Theta}^{\top}-\Theta\Theta^{\top}),\widetilde{Q}\rangle.

(16)

In Lemma
4
, we aim to show equation (
13
) is the main term which is asymptotic normal. In Lemma
5
, Lemma
6
and Lemma
7
, we show equation (
14
), (
15
) and (
16
) are negligible higher order terms, respectively.

Lemma 4
.

Under the conditions in Theorem
3
,

\displaystyle\frac{\left(\langle LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{1}R_{\perp}R_{\perp}^{\top},Q\rangle+\langle L_{\perp}L_{\perp}^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{1}RR^{\top},Q\rangle+\langle LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{1}RR^{\top},Q\rangle\right)}{\sigma S\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}/T^{1-\gamma}}}\rightarrow N(0,1)

where
S^{2}=1/T^{\gamma}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{1}}{\mathcal{P}}_{M}(Q)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C_{\gamma}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{0}}{\mathcal{P}}_{M}(Q)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
.

Lemma 5
.

Under the conditions in Theorem
3
,

\displaystyle\frac{\left|\langle LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{2}R_{\perp}R_{\perp}^{\top},Q\rangle+\langle L_{\perp}L_{\perp}^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{2}RR^{\top},Q\rangle+\langle LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{2}RR^{\top},Q\rangle\right|}{\sigma S\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}/T^{1-\gamma}}}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}0.

Lemma 6
.

Under the conditions in Theorem
3
,

\displaystyle\frac{\big|\langle\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}\widehat{Z}\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top},Q\rangle-\langle LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}RR^{\top},Q\rangle\big|}{\sigma S\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}/T^{1-\gamma}}}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}0.

Lemma 7
.

Under the conditions in Theorem
3
,

\displaystyle\frac{\big|\sum_{k=2}^{\infty}\langle(\mathcal{S}_{A,k}(\widehat{E})A\Theta\Theta^{\top}+\Theta\Theta^{\top}A\mathcal{S}_{A,k}(\widehat{E})),\widetilde{Q}\rangle\big|}{\sigma S\sqrt{d_{1}d_{1}/T^{1-\gamma}}}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}0,

\displaystyle\frac{\big|\langle(\widehat{\Theta}\widehat{\Theta}^{\top}-\Theta\Theta^{\top})A(\widehat{\Theta}\widehat{\Theta}^{\top}-\Theta\Theta^{\top}),\widetilde{Q}\rangle\big|}{\sigma S\sqrt{d_{1}d_{1}/T^{1-\gamma}}}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}0.

Combine Lemma
4
-
7
, we can conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{\langle\widehat{M},Q\rangle-\langle M,Q\rangle}{\sigma S\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}/T^{1-\gamma}}}\rightarrow N(0,1).

∎

C.4
Proof of Theorem
4

Proof.

Without loss of generality, we only prove the
i=1
case. As long as we show
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}\sigma^{2}
and
\widehat{S}^{2}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}S^{2}
, then by Slutsky’s Theorem, we can obtain the desired result.

\mathbf{\widehat{\sigma}^{2}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}\sigma^{2}}
Notice that,
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}
can be written as

\displaystyle\widehat{\sigma}^{2}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{b}{T}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\langle M-\widehat{M}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle^{2}}_{I}+\underbrace{\frac{2b}{T}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\langle M-\widehat{M}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle\xi_{t}}_{II}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\frac{b}{T}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\xi_{t}^{2}}_{III}.

For term I,

\displaystyle\big|\frac{b}{T}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\langle M-\widehat{M}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle^{2}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{t^{\gamma}}{T}\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|_{\max}^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{T^{1-\gamma}}\sigma^{2},

and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{b^{2}}{T^{2}}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\langle M-\widehat{M}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle^{4}\big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{b^{2}}{T^{2}}\frac{2}{\varepsilon_{t}}\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|_{\max}^{2}\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{t^{\gamma}}{T^{2}}\sigma^{4}.

By martingale Bernstein inequality, with probability at least
1-d_{1}^{-2}
,

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg|\frac{b}{T}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\langle M-\widehat{M}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle^{2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\bigg|+\sqrt{\frac{\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{d_{1}r\sigma^{2}\log^{4}d_{1}\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)}{T^{1-\gamma}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\sigma^{2},

which indicates
I\overset{p}{\rightarrow}0
when
\frac{d_{1}\log^{4}d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}\rightarrow 0
.

Similarly, for term
II
, note that
|\xi_{t}|
is bounded with
\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{1}
with high probability,

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{2b}{T}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\langle M-\widehat{M}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle\xi_{t}\bigg|\leq\frac{2b}{T}\frac{2}{\varepsilon_{t}}\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|_{\max}\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{1}\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\log^{1/2}d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}},

and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{4b^{2}}{T^{2}}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\langle M-\widehat{M}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle^{2}\xi_{t}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\lesssim\frac{t^{\gamma}\sigma^{2}}{T^{2}}\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|_{\max}^{2}\lesssim\frac{t^{\gamma}\sigma^{4}}{T^{2}}.

By martingale Bernstein inequality, with probability at least
1-d_{1}^{-2}
,

\displaystyle\bigg|II-\frac{2b}{T}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\langle M-\widehat{M}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle\xi_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\bigg|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\sigma^{2},

which indicates
II\overset{p}{\rightarrow}0
as long as
\frac{\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}\rightarrow 0
.

Lastly, for term
III
,

\displaystyle\|\frac{b}{T}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\xi_{t}^{2}\|_{\psi_{1}}\lesssim\frac{t^{\gamma}\sigma^{2}}{T},

and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{b^{2}}{T^{2}}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\xi_{t}^{4}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\lesssim\frac{t^{\gamma}\sigma^{4}}{T}.

By martingale Bernstein inequality, with probability at least
1-d_{1}^{-2}
,

\displaystyle\bigg|III-\frac{b}{T}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\xi_{t}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\bigg|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\sigma^{2},

which indicates
III\overset{p}{\rightarrow}\sigma^{2}
as long as
\frac{\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}\rightarrow 0
.

Showing
\widehat{S}^{2}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}S^{2}
.
Notice that

\displaystyle\left|\|\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\|\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{M}}(Q)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\|L^{\top}Q\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\|L_{\perp}^{\top}QR\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\|\widehat{L}^{\top}Q\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\|\widehat{L}_{\perp}^{\top}Q\widehat{R}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\|L^{\top}Q\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\|\widehat{L}^{\top}Q\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\right|+\left|\|L_{\perp}^{\top}QR\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\|\widehat{L}_{\perp}^{\top}Q\widehat{R}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\right|.

Observing that
L
and
\widehat{L}
both have orthonormal columns, then

\displaystyle\left|\|L^{\top}Q\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\|\widehat{L}^{\top}Q\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\|LL^{\top}Q\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\|\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}Q\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\|(LL^{\top}-\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top})Q\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2\left|\langle(LL^{\top}-\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top})Q,LL^{\top}Q\rangle\right|.

In the following we shall consider the upper bound under the event
{\mathcal{E}}
defined in the proof of Lemma
10
.
The probability of the following event

\displaystyle\|(LL^{\top}-\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top})Q\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\left(\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}}|Q(j_{1},j_{2})|\|(LL^{\top}-\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top})e_{j_{1}}\|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}^{2}\|LL^{\top}-\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}\|_{2,\max}^{2}\lesssim\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\frac{\mu rd_{1}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}.

approaches 1 as
\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}\rightarrow 0
, which can be easily satisfied under the conditions in Theorem
3
.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\left|\langle(LL^{\top}-\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top})Q,LL^{\top}Q\rangle\right|\leq\|L^{\top}Q\|_{\rm{F}}\|L^{\top}(LL^{\top}-\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top})Q\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\|L^{\top}Q\|_{\rm{F}}\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}\|\|LL^{\top}-\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}\|_{2,\max}\lesssim\|Q\|_{\rm{F}}\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu rd_{1}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}.

Therefore, as long as
\|Q\|_{\rm{F}}\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu rd_{1}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\rightarrow 0
, the above term converges to 0 in probability.

Next we bound
\left|\|L_{\perp}^{\top}QR\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\|\widehat{L}_{\perp}^{\top}Q\widehat{R}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\right|
. It can be decomposed into

\displaystyle\left|\|L_{\perp}^{\top}QR\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\|\widehat{L}_{\perp}^{\top}Q\widehat{R}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\right|\leq\left|\|L_{\perp}^{\top}QR\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\|{L}_{\perp}^{\top}Q\widehat{R}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\right|+\left|\|L_{\perp}^{\top}Q\widehat{R}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\|\widehat{L}_{\perp}^{\top}Q\widehat{R}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\right|.

It follows that

\displaystyle\left|\|L_{\perp}^{\top}QR\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\|{L}_{\perp}^{\top}Q\widehat{R}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\right|\leq\|{L}_{\perp}^{\top}Q(RR^{\top}-\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2\left|\langle{L}_{\perp}^{\top}Q(RR^{\top}-\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top}),L_{\perp}^{\top}QR\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\frac{\mu rd_{1}^{2}\log d_{1}}{T}+\|L_{\perp}^{\top}QR\|_{\rm{F}}\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu rd_{1}^{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}},

and

\displaystyle\quad\left|\|L_{\perp}^{\top}Q\widehat{R}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\|\widehat{L}_{\perp}^{\top}Q\widehat{R}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\right|\leq\|(\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}-LL^{\top})Q\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2\left|\langle{L}_{\perp}^{\top}Q(RR^{\top}-\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top}),L_{\perp}^{\top}Q\widehat{R}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\frac{\mu rd_{1}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}+(\|L_{\perp}^{\top}Q{R}\|_{\rm{F}}+\|{L}_{\perp}^{\top}Q(RR^{\top}-\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top})\|_{\rm{F}})\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu rd_{1}^{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\frac{\mu rd_{1}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}+\|L_{\perp}^{\top}Q{R}\|_{\rm{F}}\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu rd_{1}^{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}+\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\frac{\mu rd_{1}^{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}.

We can show
\left|\|L_{\perp}^{\top}Q\widehat{R}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\|\widehat{L}_{\perp}^{\top}Q\widehat{R}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\right|
converges to 0 in probability as long as
\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\frac{d_{1}^{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}\rightarrow 0
.
As a result,
\|\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{M}}(Q)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}\|\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
. By Lemma
8
, it directly follows
\|\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{\Omega}_{i,T}}\mathcal{P}_{\widehat{M}}(Q)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}\|\mathcal{P}_{\Omega_{i}}\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
for
i=0,1
. Moreover,
b_{T}\rightarrow 1
when
T\rightarrow\infty
.
Then by Slutsky’s Theorem,
\widehat{S}^{2}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}S^{2}
.
∎

C.5
Proof of Corollary
1

Proof.

Note that

\displaystyle(\langle\widehat{M}_{1},Q\rangle-\langle\widehat{M}_{0},Q\rangle)-(\langle M_{1},Q\rangle-\langle M_{0},Q\rangle)=(\langle\widehat{M}_{1},Q\rangle-\langle M_{1},Q\rangle)-(\langle\widehat{M}_{0},Q\rangle-\langle M_{0},Q\rangle),

then
\langle\widehat{M}_{1},Q\rangle-\langle M_{1},Q\rangle
and
\langle\widehat{M}_{0},Q\rangle-\langle M_{0},Q\rangle
can be decomposed into main term and negligible terms in the same way as Theorem
3
. The upper bound of all the negligible terms for both
i=0
and 1 still follow the Lemma
5
-
7
. The main CLT term is

\displaystyle\frac{b}{T}\sum_{t_{1}=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t_{1}}=1)}{\pi_{t_{1}}}\xi_{t_{1}}\langle X_{t_{1}},\mathcal{P}_{M_{1}}(Q)\rangle-\frac{b}{T}\sum_{t_{2}=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t_{2}}=0)}{1-\pi_{t_{2}}}\xi_{t_{2}}\langle X_{t_{2}},\mathcal{P}_{M_{0}}(Q)\rangle.

As long as we show
\sum_{t_{1}=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t_{1}}=1)}{\pi_{t_{1}}}\xi_{t_{1}}\langle X_{t_{1}},\mathcal{P}_{M_{1}}(Q)\rangle
is uncorrelated with

\sum_{t_{2}=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t_{2}}=0)}{1-\pi_{t_{2}}}\xi_{t_{2}}\langle X_{t_{2}},\mathcal{P}_{M_{0}}(Q)\rangle
, then the asymptotic variance is the sum of their individual variance, i.e.,
\sigma_{1}^{2}S_{1}^{2}+\sigma_{0}^{2}S_{0}^{2}
. Notice that,

\displaystyle\quad\frac{b^{2}}{T^{2}}\sum_{t_{1}=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t_{1}}=1)}{\pi_{t_{1}}}\xi_{t_{1}}\langle X_{t_{1}},\mathcal{P}_{M_{1}}(Q)\rangle\sum_{t_{2}=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t_{2}}=0)}{1-\pi_{t_{2}}}\xi_{t_{2}}\langle X_{t_{2}},\mathcal{P}_{M_{0}}(Q)\rangle

\displaystyle=\frac{b^{2}d_{1}^{2}d_{2}^{2}}{T^{2}}\sum_{t_{1}=T_{0}+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t_{1}}=1)\mathbbm{1}(a_{t_{2}}=0)}{\pi_{t_{1}}(1-\pi_{t_{2}})}\xi_{t_{1}}\xi_{t_{2}}\langle X_{t_{1}},\mathcal{P}_{M_{1}}(Q)\rangle\langle X_{t_{2}},\mathcal{P}_{M_{0}}(Q)\rangle.

When
t_{1}=t_{2}
,
\mathbbm{1}(a_{t_{1}}=1)\mathbbm{1}(a_{t_{2}}=0)=0
. When
t_{1}\neq t_{2}
,
{\mathbb{E}}[\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t_{1}}=1)\mathbbm{1}(a_{t_{2}}=0)}{\pi_{t_{1}}(1-\pi_{t_{2}})}\xi_{t_{1}}\xi_{t_{2}}X_{t_{1}}X_{t_{2}}]=0
due to the i.i.d. distributed
\xi_{t}
. As a result, the two terms are uncorrelated.

Together with Lemma
5
-
7
, all the negligible terms converge to 0 when divided by

\sqrt{(\sigma_{1}^{2}S_{1}^{2}+\sigma_{0}^{2}S_{0}^{2})d_{1}d_{2}/T^{1-\gamma}}
. Then similar as Theorem
4
, we can replace
\sigma_{1}^{2}S_{1}^{2}+\sigma_{0}^{2}S_{0}^{2}
with
\widehat{\sigma}_{1}^{2}\widehat{S}_{1}^{2}+\widehat{\sigma}_{0}^{2}\widehat{S}_{0}^{2}
and conclude the proof.
∎

Appendix D
Proofs of Technical Lemmas

D.1
Proof of Lemma
1

Proof.

Step 1: uniform bound for
\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))A_{\tau}
and
\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))B_{\tau}
.

For
\forall

1\leq\tau\leq t-1
, under
{\mathcal{E}}_{\tau-1}
, by inequality
(a+b+c)^{2}\leq 3(a^{2}+b^{2}+c^{3})
,

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{\tau}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{24}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\eta_{\tau}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{t})^{2}\max(\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{2,\max}^{2},\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{2,\max}^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{48}{\varepsilon_{t}^{4}}\eta_{\tau}^{4}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau})^{4}\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{\max}^{2}.

Since
\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa^{2}}{d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lesssim\mu_{0}r\kappa^{2}\prod_{k=1}^{\tau-1}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{d_{2}}+\\
\sum_{k=1}^{\tau-1}\frac{\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}\eta_{k}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}^{2}}
. And by the property of subGaussian random variable,
\max|\xi_{\tau}|<C\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{1}
for some constant
C
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{\tau}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}\left(\mu_{0}r\kappa^{2}\prod_{k=1}^{\tau-1}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{d_{2}}+\sum_{k=1}^{\tau-1}\frac{\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}\eta_{k}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}^{2}}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{1}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{4}}\frac{\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3}\kappa^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}\left(\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}\left(\prod_{k=1}^{\tau-1}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{d_{2}}+\sum_{k=1}^{\tau-1}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}^{2}}\right)+\sigma^{4}\log^{2}d_{1}\right),

where we use
\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{4}=\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}\lesssim\frac{2\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3}\kappa^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}(\prod_{k=1}^{\tau-1}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{d_{2}}+\sum_{k=1}^{\tau-1}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}^{2}})
in the second line. The fourth order terms will be dominated as long as
\eta_{\tau}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}^{1/2}}{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}}
and
\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\gg\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\log d_{1}}{d_{1}\kappa^{2}}
. So
\|\Delta_{\tau}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}(\mu_{0}r\kappa^{2}\prod_{k=1}^{\tau-1}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\|U_{0}V_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{d_{2}}+\sum_{k=1}^{\tau-1}\frac{\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}\eta_{k}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}^{2}}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{1})
.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\left|\langle\Delta_{\tau},\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\rangle\right|\leq

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{\tau}=1)}{\pi_{\tau}}\eta_{\tau}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau})\langle X_{\tau},(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{\tau}=1)}{\pi_{\tau}}\eta_{t}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau})\langle X_{\tau},\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)\rangle\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{\tau}=1)}{\pi_{\tau}^{2}}\eta_{\tau}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau})^{2}\langle X_{\tau}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}X_{\tau},\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\rangle\right|.

For term
\langle X_{\tau},(U_{\tau-1}V_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\rangle
, we have

\displaystyle\langle X_{\tau},(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\rangle=\langle X_{\tau}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top},\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\rangle

\displaystyle\quad\leq\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{2,\max}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}.

By symmetry,
\langle X_{\tau},\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)\rangle\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}
. In addition,

\displaystyle\langle X_{\tau}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}X_{\tau},\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{\max}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\quad|\langle\Delta_{\tau},\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\rangle|\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}(\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}+\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{1})\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}(\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{1})\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}}{\varepsilon_{t}}\left(\mu_{0}^{1/2}r^{1/2}\kappa\prod_{k=1}^{\tau-1}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})^{1/2}\frac{\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sqrt{d_{2}}}+\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{\tau-1}\frac{\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}\eta_{k}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}^{2}}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad+\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{1}\right)\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\left(\mu_{0}r\kappa^{2}\prod_{k=1}^{\tau-1}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{d_{2}}+\sum_{k=1}^{\tau-1}\frac{\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}\eta_{k}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}^{2}}+\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}\right)\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}\log d_{1}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\left(\prod_{k=1}^{\tau-1}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{\tau-1}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}}\right)\log^{-1}d_{1}.

By dealing
\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}^{3/2}r^{3/2}\kappa\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}}(\prod_{k=1}^{\tau-1}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})^{1/2}\frac{\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sqrt{d_{2}}}+\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{\tau-1}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}^{2}}})
as before, the second inequality holds since the second order term will be dominated as long as
\eta_{\tau}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}^{1/2}}{\mu_{0}r\kappa\lambda_{\max}}
and
\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\gg\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\log d_{1}}{d_{1}\kappa^{2}}
. And the third inequality holds by
2ab\leq a^{2}+b^{2}
.

As a result, we have the uniform bound of
\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))A_{\tau}
for
1\leq\tau\leq t-1
,

\displaystyle\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))A_{\tau}\lesssim\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))\left(\mu_{0}r\kappa^{2}\prod_{k=1}^{\tau-1}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{d_{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+\sum_{k=1}^{\tau-1}\frac{\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}\eta_{k}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}^{2}}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{1}\right)\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}\log d_{1}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))\left(\prod_{k=1}^{\tau-1}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{\tau-1}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}}\right)\log^{-1}d_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{\tau}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}\log d_{1}\left(\mu_{0}r\kappa^{2}\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{d_{2}}+\sum_{k=1}^{t-1}\frac{\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}\eta_{k}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}^{2}}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{1}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\left(\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}}\right)\log^{-1}d_{1}:=R_{A}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))B_{\tau}\lesssim\max_{\tau}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}\left(\mu_{0}r\kappa^{2}\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{d_{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}\eta_{k}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}^{2}}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{1}\right):=R_{B}.

Obviously, the uniform bounds for
A_{t}
and
B_{t}
are the same as above.

Step 2: calculating the variance.

Denote the variance of
\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))A_{\tau}
as
\sigma_{A\tau}^{2}
,

\displaystyle\sigma_{A\tau}^{2}\lesssim\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{\tau}=1)}{\pi_{\tau}^{2}}\eta_{\tau}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau})^{2}\langle X_{\tau},(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\rangle^{2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\tau-1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{\tau}=1)}{\pi_{\tau}^{2}}\eta_{\tau}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau})^{2}\langle X_{\tau},\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)\rangle^{2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\tau-1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{\tau}=1)}{\pi_{\tau}^{4}}\eta_{\tau}^{4}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau})^{4}\langle X_{\tau}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}X_{\tau},\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\rangle^{2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\tau-1}\right].

For the first expectation, we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{\tau}=1)}{\pi_{\tau}^{2}}\eta_{\tau}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau})^{2}\langle X_{\tau},(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\rangle^{2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\tau-1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\left|\langle X_{\tau}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top},\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\rangle\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}{\mathbb{E}}[\xi_{\tau}^{2}]{\mathbb{E}}\left(\langle X_{\tau},(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\rangle^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{\tau-1}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{2,\max}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2\eta_{\tau}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}\left((\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\right)_{ij}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}+\frac{2\eta_{\tau}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}+\frac{2\eta_{\tau}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|^{2}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}+\frac{2\eta_{\tau}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\frac{O(\lambda_{\max}^{2})}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

By symmetry,

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{\tau}=1)}{\pi_{\tau}^{2}}\eta_{\tau}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau})^{2}\langle X_{\tau},\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)\rangle^{2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\tau-1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}O(\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}})+\frac{2\eta_{\tau}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\frac{O(\lambda_{\max}^{2})}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

And the last expectation is

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{\tau}=1)}{\pi_{\tau}^{4}}\eta_{\tau}^{4}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau})^{4}\langle X_{\tau}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}X_{\tau},\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\rangle^{2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\tau-1}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}}\left(\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)^{4}+\sigma^{4}\right)\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{\max}^{2}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
