\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}}\left(\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)^{4}+\sigma^{4}\right)\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}.

This gives,

\displaystyle\sigma_{A\tau}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))^{2}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}+\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))^{2}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

The fourth order terms is dominated as long as
\eta_{\tau}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}^{1/2}}{\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}\lambda_{\max}}
and
\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\gg\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}\kappa^{2}}
. Since
\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\prod_{k=1}^{\tau-1}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{\tau-1}\frac{c_{2}\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}}
, we can further obtain

\displaystyle\sigma_{A\tau}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}}\left(\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})^{2}\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}+\left(\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{c_{2}\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}}\right)^{2}\right)\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{c_{2}\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}}.

As a result,

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sigma_{A\tau}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}}\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}O(\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}})

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}}\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{c_{2}\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}}O(\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}})

\displaystyle\quad+\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\log^{-1/2}d_{1}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{3/2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}},

where we apply inequality
2ab\leq a^{2}+b^{2}
to term
\sqrt{\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}}\sqrt{\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}}\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})^{1/2}\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}
.

Similarly, denote the variance of
\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))B_{\tau}
as
\sigma_{B\tau}^{2}
, it can be shown that,

\displaystyle\sigma_{B\tau}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))^{2}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left[\langle\widehat{U}_{{\tau}-1}\widehat{V}_{{\tau}-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle^{4}|\mathcal{F}_{\tau-1}\right]\max\left(\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{2,\max}^{4},\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{2,\max}^{4}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+{\mathbb{E}}[\xi_{\tau}^{4}]\max\left({\mathbb{E}}\left[\|X_{\tau}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}|\mathcal{F}_{\tau-1}\right],{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}X_{\tau}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}|\mathcal{F}_{\tau-1}\right]\right)\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))^{2}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}}\left(\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{{\tau}-1}\widehat{V}_{{\tau}-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\|\widehat{U}_{{\tau}-1}\widehat{V}_{{\tau}-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{2}^{2}}+\sigma^{4}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{2}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}}\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}^{2}}\left(\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}}\right)^{2}\frac{\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3}\kappa^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}}\sigma^{4}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{2}^{2}},

where the second term in the second last inequality comes from

{\mathbb{E}}\left(\|X_{\tau}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}|\mathcal{F}_{\tau-1}\right)\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left(\|X_{\tau}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{\tau-1}\right)\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{2,\max}^{2}\leq\frac{rO(\lambda_{\max}^{2})}{d_{2}}O(\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}).

And in the last inequality we again use

\displaystyle\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa^{2}}{d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}r\kappa^{2}\prod_{k=1}^{\tau-1}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}}{d_{2}}+\sum_{k=1}^{\tau-1}\frac{\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}\eta_{k}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}^{2}}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sigma_{B\tau}^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}}}\frac{1}{d_{1}d_{2}^{1/2}}(\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}})\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}+\sqrt{\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{d_{2}}.

Step 3: Proof of concentration.

From the calculation in
Step 2
,

R_{A}\log d_{1}+\sqrt{\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sigma_{A\tau}^{2}\log d_{1}}\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{c_{2}\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}}

and

R_{B}\log d_{1}+\sqrt{\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sigma_{B\tau}^{2}\log d_{1}}\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{c_{2}\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}},

as long as: (1).
\max_{\tau\leq t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\leq\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}
; (2).
\eta_{\tau}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{2}}{\sqrt{\mu_{0}r}\lambda_{\max}\log^{1/2}d_{1}}
; (3).
\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\lesssim\frac{d_{1}d_{2}^{2}}{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\log d_{1}}
; (4).
\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}}\lesssim\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}^{3}}{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{4}\log d_{1}}
; (5).
\sqrt{\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}}}\lesssim\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}
.
Under the stepsize and SNR conditions in Equation
11
, all of them can be satisfied.

By martingale Bernstein inequality, with probability at least
1-d_{1}^{-200}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))A_{\tau}+A_{t}\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}},

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))B_{\tau}+B_{t}\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}}.

∎

D.2
Proof of Lemma
2

Proof.

For any
l
in
[d_{1}]
,

\displaystyle\|e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{t}\widehat{V}_{t}^{\top}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-\Delta_{t})(\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}-\Delta_{t}^{\top})e_{l}=\|e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\|^{2}-2e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}+\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}.

This leads to

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\|e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{t}\widehat{V}_{t}^{\top}\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]=\|e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\|^{2}-2{\mathbb{E}}[e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]+{\mathbb{E}}[\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}].

We first compute
2{\mathbb{E}}[e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]
. Denote
\delta_{ij}=\mathbbm{1}(i=j)
, then

\displaystyle 2{\mathbb{E}}(e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1})=2{\mathbb{E}}\left[e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\eta_{t}(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})X_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+2{\mathbb{E}}\left[e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\eta_{t}(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}X_{t}V_{t-1}U_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad-2{\mathbb{E}}\left[e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\eta_{t}^{2}(\langle U_{t-1}V_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})^{2}X_{t}V_{t-1}U_{t-1}^{\top}X_{t}V_{t-1}U_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2\eta_{t}}{d_{1}d_{2}}e_{l}^{\top}(U_{t-1}V_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}+\frac{2\eta_{t}}{d_{1}d_{2}}e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{4\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}\delta_{il}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)_{ij}^{2}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top})_{ij}^{2}-\frac{4\eta_{t}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}\delta_{il}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top})_{ij}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta_{t}\lambda_{\min}}{d_{1}d_{2}}e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}-\frac{4\eta_{t}\lambda_{\max}}{d_{1}d_{2}}e_{l}^{\top}M\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}+\frac{\eta_{t}\lambda_{\min}}{d_{1}d_{2}}e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{4\eta_{t}\lambda_{\max}}{d_{1}d_{2}}e_{l}^{\top}M\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}-\frac{4\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{1}^{2}}-\frac{4\eta_{t}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2\eta_{t}\lambda_{\min}}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\|^{2}-\frac{8\eta_{t}\lambda_{\max}}{d_{1}d_{2}}\frac{\sqrt{\mu\mu_{0}}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}-\frac{4\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{1}^{2}}-\frac{4\eta_{t}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}.

The second order term in the second last inequality comes from
\sum_{ij}\delta_{il}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)_{ij}^{2}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top})_{ij}^{2}\leq\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{2,\max}^{2}\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\|_{2,\max}^{2}
. And the other term is similar. The last inequality holds since
e_{l}^{\top}M\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}\leq\|e_{l}^{\top}M\|\|\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}\|
and
\|e_{l}^{\top}M\|\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}}
while
\|\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}\|\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}}
.

Then we compute
{\mathbb{E}}(\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1})
. Since
(a+b+c)^{2}\leq 3a^{2}+3b^{2}+3c^{2}
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}(\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1})

\displaystyle\leq 3{\mathbb{E}}\left[e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\eta_{t}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})^{2}X_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}X_{t}^{\top}e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+3{\mathbb{E}}\left[e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\eta_{t}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})^{2}\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}X_{t}X_{t}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+3{\mathbb{E}}\left[e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{4}}\eta_{t}^{4}(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})^{4}X_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}X_{t}X_{t}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}X_{t}^{\top}e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right].

The first term can be written as,

\displaystyle 3{\mathbb{E}}\left[e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\eta_{t}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})^{2}X_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}X_{t}^{\top}e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{6\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)_{ij}^{2}\delta_{il}\|e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\|^{2}+\frac{6\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}\sigma^{2}\sum_{ij}\delta_{il}\|e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{6\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}+\frac{6\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{d_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{6\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{1}d_{2}}+\frac{6\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{d_{2}}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle 3{\mathbb{E}}\left[e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\eta_{t}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})^{2}\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}X_{t}X_{t}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{6\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)_{ij}^{2}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top})_{li}^{2}+\frac{6\eta_{t}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top})_{li}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{6\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{1}^{2}}+\frac{6\eta_{t}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}.

And the last fourth order term can be bounded in the same way. Moreover, the first order term
\frac{8\eta_{t}\lambda_{\max}}{d_{1}d_{2}}\frac{\sqrt{\mu\mu_{0}}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}
will dominate all the higher order terms as long as
\eta_{t}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{t}d_{2}}{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}}
and
\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\gg\frac{\eta_{t}}{\varepsilon_{t}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}d_{1}}{\kappa}
. So the sum of all the higher order terms will be less than
\frac{2\eta_{t}\lambda_{\max}}{d_{1}d_{2}}\frac{\sqrt{\mu\mu_{0}}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}
. Recall
f(\eta_{t})=\frac{\eta_{t}\lambda_{\min}}{d_{1}d_{2}}
. Overall, we have

\displaystyle\|e_{l}^{\top}U_{t}V_{t}^{\top}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\|e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\|^{2}-2e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}+\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\|e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\|^{2}-2(e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}-{\mathbb{E}}(e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}))

\displaystyle\quad+(\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}(\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}))-2{\mathbb{E}}(e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1})+{\mathbb{E}}(\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1})

\displaystyle\leq(1-f(\eta_{t}))\|e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\|^{2}+\frac{10\eta_{t}\sqrt{\mu\mu_{0}}r\lambda_{\max}^{3}}{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{2({\mathbb{E}}(e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1})-e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l})}_{A_{t}}+\underbrace{(\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}(\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}))}_{B_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq(1-f(\eta_{t}))(1-f(\eta_{t-1}))\|e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{t-2}\widehat{V}_{t-2}^{\top}\|^{2}+(1-f(\eta_{t-1}))\frac{10\eta_{t-1}\sqrt{\mu\mu_{0}}r\lambda_{\max}^{3}}{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}}+\frac{10\eta_{t}\sqrt{\mu\mu_{0}}r\lambda_{\max}^{3}}{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+(1-f(\eta_{t}))(A_{t-1}+B_{t-1})+(A_{t}+B_{t})

\displaystyle\leq\prod_{\tau=1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{\tau}))\|e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}\|^{2}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{10\eta_{\tau}\sqrt{\mu\mu_{0}}r\lambda_{\max}^{3}}{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}A_{\tau}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}B_{\tau}.

It can be shown that the first term is less than
\frac{1}{6}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}
. The second term will be less than
\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}
as long as
\mu_{0}=100\mu
and
\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\eta_{\tau}\leq\frac{d_{1}d_{2}}{2\lambda_{\max}}
, which is satisfied under the stepsize condition in Theorem
1
. Then we need to show
\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}A_{\tau}\leq\frac{1}{6}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}
and
\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}B_{\tau}\leq\frac{1}{6}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}
.

Similar as the proof of Lemma
1
, we first derive the uniform bound for
A_{\tau}
and
B_{\tau}
. Under
\mathcal{E}_{\tau-1}
, obviously,
\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}\lesssim\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}
. We use this trivial bound in the following calculation.

\displaystyle|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{\tau}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}e_{l}|

\displaystyle\leq|e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{\tau}=1)}{\pi_{\tau}}\eta_{\tau}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau})X_{\tau}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}e_{l}|

\displaystyle\quad+|e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{\tau}=1)}{\pi_{\tau}}\eta_{\tau}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau})\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}X_{\tau}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}e_{l}|

\displaystyle\quad+|e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{\tau}=1)}{\pi_{\tau}^{2}}\eta_{\tau}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau})^{2}X_{\tau}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}X_{\tau}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}e_{l}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta_{\tau}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}|\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau}|\max_{j}|e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}e_{l}|

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2\eta_{\tau}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}|\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau}|\|e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}X_{\tau}\|\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}e_{l}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}|\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau}|^{2}\max_{j}\|e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}X_{\tau}\|\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}e_{l}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta_{\tau}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}(\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}+\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{1})\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2\eta_{\tau}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}(\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}+\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{1})\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}(\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{1})\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}(\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}}+\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{1})\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}}.

The first order term dominates as long as
\eta_{\tau}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{\tau}\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}}{\lambda_{\max}}
and
\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\gg\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{2}\log d_{1}}{\kappa}
. Then

\displaystyle A_{\tau}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{\tau}\frac{\eta_{\tau}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}(\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}}+\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{1})O(\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}})O(\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}}):=R_{A}.

For
\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{\tau}\|^{2}
, we also have

\displaystyle\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{\tau}\|^{2}\lesssim|e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{\tau}=1)}{\pi_{\tau}^{2}}\eta_{\tau}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau})^{2}X_{\tau}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}X_{\tau}^{\top}e_{l}|

\displaystyle\quad+|e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{\tau}=1)}{\pi_{\tau}^{2}}\eta_{\tau}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau})^{2}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}X_{\tau}X_{\tau}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}e_{l}|

\displaystyle\quad+|e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{\tau}=1)}{\pi_{\tau}^{4}}\eta_{\tau}^{4}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau})^{4}X_{\tau}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}X_{\tau}X_{\tau}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}X_{\tau}^{\top}e_{l}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}|\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t}|^{2}\max_{j}|e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}e_{j}|

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}|\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau}|^{2}\|e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}X_{\tau}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{4}}|\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau}|^{4}\max_{j}\|e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}X_{\tau}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}(\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{1})\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}(\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{1})\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{4}}(\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{4}+\sigma^{4}\log^{2}d_{1})\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}(\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{1})\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}^{2}}.

The second order term dominates as long as
\eta_{\tau}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}}{\lambda_{\max}}
and
\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\gg\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{2}\log d_{1}}{\kappa^{2}}
. Then

\displaystyle B_{\tau}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{\tau}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}(\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{1})O(\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}^{2}}):=R_{B}.

The variance of
A_{\tau}
and
B_{\tau}
, denoted as
\sigma_{A\tau}^{2}
and
\sigma_{B\tau}^{2}
, can be calculated as before.

\displaystyle\sigma_{A\tau}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)_{ij}^{2}\delta_{il}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{jl}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)_{ij}^{2}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{li}^{2}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{jl}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}\delta_{il}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{jl}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{li}^{2}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{jl}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}\sum_{ij}\delta_{il}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{jl}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{\max}^{2}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{jl}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}\delta_{il}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{jl}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{\max}^{2}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{jl}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{1}}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{2}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{1}}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}},

where we apply
\sum_{ij}\delta_{il}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{jl}^{2}=\|e_{l}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|^{2}\leq\|e_{l}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|^{2}\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|^{2}\\
\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}\lambda_{\max}^{2}
in the last inequality.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\sigma_{B\tau}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)_{ij}^{4}\delta_{il}\|e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|^{4}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}d_{2}}\sigma^{4}\sum_{ij}\delta_{il}\|e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|^{4}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)_{ij}^{4}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{li}^{4}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}\sigma^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{li}^{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{4}\sum_{ij}\delta_{il}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}\sigma^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}\delta_{il}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{4}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{\max}^{2}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{li}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}\sigma^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{\max}^{2}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{li}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}\sigma^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}\sigma^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}.

From the calculation above,
R_{A}\log d+\sqrt{\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sigma_{A\tau}^{2}\log d_{1}}\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}
and
R_{B}\log d+\sqrt{\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sigma_{B\tau}^{2}\log d_{1}}\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}
as long as 1.
\max_{\tau}\frac{\eta_{\tau}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\lesssim\frac{d_{2}^{3/2}}{\sqrt{\mu_{0}r}\log d_{1}\lambda_{\max}}
, 2.
\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\gg\max_{\tau}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\log^{3}d_{1}}{d_{2}^{3}\kappa^{2}}
, 3.
\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{1}}\lesssim\frac{d_{1}d_{2}^{2}}{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{4}}
, 4.
\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\gg\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}\kappa^{2}}
, 5.
\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}}\lesssim\frac{d_{1}d_{2}^{4}}{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{4}}
, 6.
\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\gg\sqrt{\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{2}\kappa^{4}}}
. All of them can be satisfied under the stepsize and SNR conditions in Equation
11
.

As a result, by Bernstein inequality, with probability at least
1-d_{1}^{-200}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}A_{\tau}\leq\frac{1}{6}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}.

And with probability at least
1-d_{1}^{-200}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}B_{\tau}\leq\frac{1}{6}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}.

Finally, combine all the results above and the union bound, we can obtain that with probability at least
1-d_{1}^{-200}
,

\displaystyle\|e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{t}V_{t}^{\top}\|^{2}\leq\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}.

By symmetry, following the same arguments, we have for
\forall j\in[d_{2}]
, with the same probability,

\displaystyle\|e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{t}\widehat{U}_{t}^{\top}\|^{2}\leq\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}}.

∎

D.3
Proof of Lemma
3

Proof.

For any
l
in
[d_{1}]
,

\displaystyle\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t}\widehat{V}_{t}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}

\displaystyle=e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M-\Delta_{t})(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M^{\top}-\Delta_{t}^{\top})e_{l}

\displaystyle=\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}-2e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}(\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}+\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}.

This leads to

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}(\|e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{t}\widehat{V}_{t}^{\top}\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1})=\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}-2{\mathbb{E}}[e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}(\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]+{\mathbb{E}}[\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}].

We first compute
2{\mathbb{E}}[e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}(\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]
.

\displaystyle\quad\quad 2{\mathbb{E}}[e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}(\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]

\displaystyle=2{\mathbb{E}}\left[e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\eta_{t}(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})X_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}(\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+2{\mathbb{E}}\left[e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\eta_{t}(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}X_{t}(\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad-2{\mathbb{E}}\left[e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\eta_{t}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top},X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{t})^{2}X_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}X_{t}(\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2\eta_{t}}{d_{1}d_{2}}e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}(\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2\eta_{t}}{d_{1}d_{2}}e_{l}^{\top}\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)(\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{4\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}\delta_{il}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)_{ij}^{3}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top})_{ij}-\frac{4\eta_{t}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}\delta_{il}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top})_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)_{ij}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\eta_{t}\lambda_{\min}}{d_{1}d_{2}}e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)(\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}+\frac{\eta_{t}\lambda_{\min}}{d_{1}d_{2}}e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)(\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{4\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}-\frac{4\eta_{t}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2\eta_{t}\lambda_{\min}}{d_{1}d_{2}}\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}-\frac{4\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}O(\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}})\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}-\frac{4\eta_{t}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}.

The first second order term in the second last inequality comes from
\sum_{ij}\delta_{il}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)_{ij}^{3}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top})_{ij}\leq\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}\|\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}\|_{\max}\sum_{ij}\delta_{il}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)_{ij}^{2}\leq O(\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}})\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}
. And the other term is similar.

For
{\mathbb{E}}(\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1})
, analogous to the calculation before, we also have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}(\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1})\lesssim\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}+\frac{\eta_{t}^{2}\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}.

Recall
f(\eta_{t})=\frac{\eta_{t}\lambda_{\min}}{2d_{1}d_{2}}
. Then for some constant
c_{2}
,

\displaystyle-2{\mathbb{E}}(e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1})+{\mathbb{E}}(\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1})

\displaystyle\leq-2f(\eta_{t})\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}+\frac{c_{2}\eta_{t}^{2}\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq-f(\eta_{t})\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}+\frac{c_{2}\eta_{t}^{2}\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}.

We again use telescoping to derive

\displaystyle\|e_{l}^{\top}(U_{t}V_{t}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}=\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}-2e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}(\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}+\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad=\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}-2(e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}(\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}-{\mathbb{E}}(e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}(\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}))

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\left(\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}[\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]\right)-2{\mathbb{E}}[e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]+{\mathbb{E}}[\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]

\displaystyle\quad\leq(1-f(\eta_{t}))\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-1}\widehat{V}_{t-1}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}+\frac{c_{2}\eta_{t}^{2}\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\underbrace{2({\mathbb{E}}(e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}(\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1})-e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}(\widehat{V}_{t-1}\widehat{U}_{t-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l})}_{A_{t}}+\underbrace{(\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}[\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{t}\|^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}])}_{B_{t}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq(1-f(\eta_{t}))(1-f(\eta_{t-1}))\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t-2}\widehat{V}_{t-2}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}+(1-f(\eta_{t}))\frac{c_{2}\eta_{t-1}^{2}\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t-1}d_{1}d_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\frac{c_{2}\eta_{t}^{2}\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}+(1-f(\eta_{t}))(A_{t-1}+B_{t-1})+(A_{t}+B_{t})

\displaystyle\quad\leq\prod_{\tau=1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{\tau}))\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))\frac{c_{2}\eta_{\tau}^{2}\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\frac{c_{2}\eta_{t}^{2}\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}d_{1}d_{2}}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))A_{\tau}+A_{t}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))B_{\tau}+B_{t}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\prod_{\tau=1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{\tau}))\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{c_{2}\eta_{\tau}^{2}\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))A_{\tau}+A_{t}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))B_{\tau}+B_{t}.

The first term
\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}}
under the incoherence condition of
U_{0}
and
V_{0}
.

Next, we derive the uniform bound for
\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))A_{\tau}
and
\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{k}))B_{\tau}
.

\displaystyle|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{\tau}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}|\leq|e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{\tau}=1)}{\pi_{\tau}}\eta_{\tau}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau})X_{\tau}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}|

\displaystyle\quad\quad+|e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{\tau}=1)}{\pi_{\tau}}\eta_{\tau}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{\tau}\rangle-\xi_{\tau})\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}X_{\tau}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}|

\displaystyle\quad\quad+|e_{l}^{\top}\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{\tau}=1)}{\pi_{\tau}^{2}}\eta_{\tau}^{2}(\langle\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M,X_{t}\rangle-\xi_{\tau})^{2}X_{\tau}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}X_{\tau}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}|

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{2\eta_{\tau}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}(\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}+\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{1})\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{\max}\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M^{\top}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\frac{2\eta_{\tau}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}(\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}+\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{1})\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{\max}\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\frac{4\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}|(\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{1})\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{\max}\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}}{d_{2}}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{1}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}}{d_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\sigma^{2}\log d_{1}\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}}{\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}}{d_{2}}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{1}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}}{d_{2}}.

The first order terms dominate as long as
\eta_{\tau}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}}{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}}
and
\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\gg\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{2}\log d_{1}}{\kappa^{2}}
.

Since
\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{\tau-1}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}+\sum_{k=1}^{\tau-1}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}^{2}}
under event
\mathcal{E}_{\tau-1}
, and applying
2ab\leq a^{2}+b^{2}
to term
\frac{\eta_{\tau}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\sigma\log^{1/2}d_{1}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}}{d_{2}}
, we have

\displaystyle\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{\tau}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})e_{l}|\lesssim\max_{\tau}\frac{\eta_{\tau}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\left(\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}^{2}}\right)\frac{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}}{d_{2}}+\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}^{2}}+\max_{\tau}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\sigma^{2}\log d_{1}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}^{2}}:=R_{A}.

Similarly, we can obtain

\displaystyle\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\|e_{l}^{\top}\Delta_{\tau}\|^{2}\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}(\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log d_{1})(\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{\max}^{2}+\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|_{\max}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\max_{\tau}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\left(\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{d_{1}d_{2}}O(\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}^{2}})+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}^{2}}\right)\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\max_{\tau}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\sigma^{2}\log d_{1}O(\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{d_{2}^{2}}):=R_{B}.

Then we calculate their conditional variance, respectively. Denote the variance of
\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{\tau}))A_{\tau}
and
\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{\tau}))B_{\tau}
as
\sigma_{A\tau}^{2}
and
\sigma_{B\tau}^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{A\tau}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{\tau}))^{2}\left(\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)_{ij}^{2}\delta_{il}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M^{\top}))_{jl}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)_{ij}^{2}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{li}^{2}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})_{jl}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}\delta_{il}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M^{\top}))_{jl}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{li}^{2}(\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M^{\top})_{jl}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{\tau}))^{2}\left(\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|^{2}\|e_{l}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{4}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{li}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|^{2}\|e_{l}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{li}^{2}\right).

Notice that,
\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{2}\|e_{l}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{d_{2}}(\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{d_{1}}+\\
\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}})^{2}
and
\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{4}\lesssim\frac{1}{d_{2}^{2}}(\prod_{k=1}^{\tau-1}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{d_{1}}\\
+\sum_{k=1}^{\tau-1}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}})^{2}
under event
\mathcal{E}_{\tau-1}
. As a result,

\displaystyle\sigma_{A\tau}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}^{2}}\left(\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{d_{1}}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}\sigma^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{1}d_{2}}\left(\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{d_{1}}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}}\right).

Similarly,

\displaystyle\sigma_{B\tau}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{\tau}))^{2}\left(\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)_{ij}^{4}\delta_{il}\|e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|^{4}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}d_{2}}\sigma^{4}\sum_{ij}\delta_{il}\|e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|^{4}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M)_{ij}^{4}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{li}^{4}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}\sigma^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{li}^{4}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{\tau}))^{2}\left(\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{4}\sum_{ij}\delta_{il}\|e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|^{4}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}\sigma^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}\delta_{il}\|e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}\|^{4}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{V}_{\tau-1}^{\top}-M\|_{\max}^{4}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{li}^{4}+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}\sigma^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}d_{1}d_{2}}\sum_{ij}(\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}\widehat{U}_{\tau-1}^{\top})_{li}^{4}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{1}d_{2}^{4}}\left(\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{d_{1}}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}}\frac{\sigma^{4}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{4}}{d_{1}d_{2}^{3}}.

From the calculation above,
R_{A}\log d_{1}+\sqrt{\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sigma_{A\tau}^{2}\log d_{1}}\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{d_{1}}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}}
and
R_{B}\log d_{1}+\sqrt{\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sigma_{B\tau}^{2}\log d_{1}}\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)\|\widehat{U}_{0}\widehat{V}_{0}^{\top}-M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{d_{1}}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}}
as long as 1.
\eta_{\tau}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{\tau}d_{2}^{3/2}}{\mu_{0}r\lambda_{\max}}
, 2.
\max\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{2}}\leq\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}
, 3.
\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}}\lesssim\frac{d_{1}d_{2}^{2}}{\lambda_{\max}^{2}\log d_{1}}
, 4.
\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}}\lesssim\frac{d_{1}d_{2}^{4}}{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{4}\log d_{1}}
, 5.
\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{4}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}^{3}}\leq(\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{\tau}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\tau}})^{2}
. All of them can be satisfied under the stepsize and SNR conditions in Equation
11
.

By Bernstein inequality, with probability at least
1-d_{1}^{-2}
,
\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{\tau}))A_{\tau}+A_{t}\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{d_{1}}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}}
and
\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\prod_{k=\tau+1}^{t}(1-f(\eta_{\tau}))B_{\tau}+B_{t}\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{d_{1}}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}}
.

Finally, we conclude that, with probability at least
1-td_{1}^{-200}
,

\displaystyle\|e_{l}^{\top}(\widehat{U}_{t}\widehat{V}_{t}^{\top}-M)\|^{2}\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{d_{1}}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{1}d_{2}}.

By symmetry, following the same arguments, we can prove with same probability, for any
j\in[d_{2}]
,

\displaystyle\|e_{j}^{\top}(\widehat{V}_{t}\widehat{U}_{t}^{\top}-M^{\top})\|^{2}\lesssim\prod_{k=1}^{t}(1-\frac{f(\eta_{k})}{2})\frac{\text{poly}(\mu,r,\kappa)\lambda_{\min}^{2}}{d_{2}}+\sum_{k=1}^{t}\frac{\eta_{k}^{2}\text{poly}(\kappa,\mu,r)\lambda_{\max}^{2}\sigma^{2}\log^{2}d_{1}}{\varepsilon_{k}d_{2}^{2}}.

∎

D.4
Proof of Lemma
4

Proof.

Denote
S^{2}=1/T^{\gamma}\|P_{\Omega_{1}}(P_{M}(Q))\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C_{\gamma}\|P_{\Omega_{0}}(P_{M}(Q))\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
. By definition,

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\frac{T^{1-\gamma}}{d_{1}d_{2}}}\left(\langle LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{1}R_{\perp}R_{\perp}^{\top},Q\rangle+\langle L_{\perp}L_{\perp}^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{1}RR^{\top},Q\rangle+\langle LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{1}RR^{\top},Q\rangle\right)}{\sigma S}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{\sqrt{\frac{1}{T^{1+\gamma}}}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{b\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\xi_{t}\langle X_{t},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle}{\sigma S}.

Next, we apply Theorem 3.2 and Corollary 3.1 in
Hall and Heyde [2014]
, the Martingale Central Limit Theorem to show the asymptotic normality.

Step 1: checking Lindeberg condition.

For any
\delta>0
,

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{b^{2}d_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\sigma^{2}S^{2}T^{1+\gamma}\pi_{t}^{2}}\xi_{t}^{2}\langle X_{t},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}\times\mathbbm{1}\left(\left|\xi_{t}\frac{\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)\langle X_{t},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle}{\sigma S\sqrt{T^{1+\gamma}}\pi_{t}}\right|>\delta\right)\bigg|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{b^{2}d_{1}d_{2}}{\sigma^{2}S^{2}T^{1+\gamma}}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\xi_{t}^{2}\langle X_{t},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}\times\mathbbm{1}\left(\left|\xi_{t}\langle X_{t},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle\right|>\frac{\sigma S\delta\sqrt{T^{1+\gamma}}\varepsilon_{t}}{2\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}}\right)\bigg|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{b^{2}d_{1}d_{2}}{\sigma^{2}S^{2}T^{1+\gamma}}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{2}{\varepsilon_{t}}\max_{X\in\mathcal{X}}\langle X,\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}\times\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathbbm{1}\left(\left|\xi_{t}\langle X_{t},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle\right|>\frac{\sigma S\delta\sqrt{T^{1+\gamma}}\varepsilon_{t}}{2\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}}\right)\right]},

where in the last inequality we use
{\mathbb{E}}[XY]\leq\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}[X^{2}]{\mathbb{E}}[Y^{2}]}
.
By incoherence condition,

\displaystyle\max_{X\in\mathcal{X}}\big|\langle X,\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle\big|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d_{2}}}\|\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\|_{\rm{F}}.

Moreover, since
\xi_{t}
is a subGaussian random variable, so the product
\xi_{t}\langle X_{t},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle
has subGaussian tail probability

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left|\xi_{t}\langle X_{t},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle\right|>\frac{\sigma S\delta\sqrt{T^{1+\gamma}}\varepsilon_{t}}{2\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}}\right)<2e^{-\frac{\sigma^{2}S^{2}\delta^{2}T^{1+\gamma}\varepsilon_{t}^{2}}{8d_{1}d_{2}\nu^{2}}},

where
\nu
is the subGaussian parameter of order
O(\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d_{2}}}\|\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\|_{\rm{F}}\sigma)
. Notice that
T^{1+\gamma}\varepsilon_{t}^{2}\leq T^{1+\gamma}\varepsilon_{T}^{2}=O(T^{1-\gamma})
. By property of exponential function,
\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\sigma^{2}}{\sigma^{2}S^{2}T^{1+\gamma}}\frac{\mu r}{d_{2}}\|\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{2}{\varepsilon_{t}}2e^{-\frac{\sigma^{2}S^{2}\delta^{2}T^{1+\gamma}\varepsilon_{t}^{2}}{16d_{1}d_{2}\nu^{2}}}
converges to 0 as long as
\frac{d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}\rightarrow 0
. Then the Lindeberg condition is satisfied.

Step 2: calculating the variance.

Next, we show the conditional variance equals to 1. Recall the definition
\Omega_{1}=\{X\in\mathcal{X}:\langle M_{1}-M_{0},X\rangle>\delta_{T,d_{1},d_{2}}\}
,
\Omega_{0}=\{X\in\mathcal{X}:\langle M_{1}-M_{0},X\rangle<\delta_{T,d_{1},d_{2}}\}
and
\Omega_{\emptyset}=\mathcal{X}\cup(\Omega_{1}\cup\Omega_{0})^{c}
. Then

\displaystyle\frac{b^{2}d_{1}d_{2}}{\sigma^{2}S^{2}T^{1+\gamma}}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\xi_{t}^{2}\langle X_{t},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}\bigg|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{b^{2}}{\sigma^{2}S^{2}T^{1+\gamma}}\sigma^{2}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\sum_{X\in\Omega_{1}}\mathbf{1}(\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X\rangle>0)\frac{1}{1-\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{2}}\langle X,\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\mathbf{1}(\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X\rangle<0)\frac{1}{\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{2}}\langle X,\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{X\in\Omega_{0}}\mathbf{1}(\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X\rangle>0)\frac{1}{1-\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{2}}\langle X,\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}+\mathbf{1}(\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X\rangle<0)\frac{1}{\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{2}}\langle X,\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{X\in\Omega_{\emptyset}}\mathbf{1}(\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X\rangle>0)\frac{1}{1-\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{2}}\langle X,\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}+\mathbf{1}(\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X\rangle<0)\frac{1}{\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{2}}\langle X,\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}.

The next Lemma shows the convergence of the indicator function.

Lemma 8
.

Given any
X\in\Omega_{1}\cup\Omega_{0}
, we have for any
T_{0}+1\leq t\leq T
, with probability at least
1-8td_{1}^{-200}
,

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}(\langle\widehat{M}_{i,t}-\widehat{M}_{1-i,t},X\rangle>0)=\mathbbm{1}(\langle M_{i}-M_{1-i},X\rangle>0).

Under this result,
\sum_{X\in\Omega_{i}}\mathbf{1}(\langle\widehat{M}_{i,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{1-i,t-1},X\rangle>0)\langle X,\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}\rightarrow\|P_{\Omega_{i}}(P_{M}(Q))\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
and
\sum_{X\in\Omega_{1-i}}\mathbf{1}(\langle\widehat{M}_{i,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{1-i,t-1},X\rangle>0)\langle X,\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}\rightarrow 0
for
i=0,1
.

Note that
\frac{b}{T}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}1/(1-\varepsilon_{t}/2)\rightarrow 1
and
\frac{b}{T^{1+\gamma}}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}2/\varepsilon_{t}\rightarrow\frac{2}{c_{2}(1+\gamma)}
when
\varepsilon_{t}=c_{2}t^{-\gamma}
. And by Assumption
1
,
\sum_{X\in\Omega_{\emptyset}}\mathbf{1}(\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X\rangle>0)\frac{1}{1-\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{2}}\langle X,\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}+\mathbf{1}(\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X\rangle<0)\frac{1}{\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{2}}\langle X,\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}\leq 2/\varepsilon_{t}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{\emptyset}}({\mathcal{P}}_{M}(Q))\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
is negligible. Then the conditional variance will converge in probability to 1. It follows

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\xi_{t}\langle X_{t},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle}{\sigma S\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}/T^{1-\gamma}}}\rightarrow N(0,1)

when
T,d_{1},d_{2}\rightarrow\infty
.
∎

D.5
Proof of Lemma
5

Proof.

By definition,

\displaystyle\langle LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{2}R_{\perp}R_{\perp}^{\top},Q\rangle+\langle L_{\perp}L_{\perp}^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{2}RR^{\top},Q\rangle+\langle LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}_{2}RR^{\top},Q\rangle=\langle\widehat{Z}_{2},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{b}{T}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\langle\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle\langle X_{t},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle-\langle\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle.

Then we prove its upper bound. For any
T_{0}+1\leq t\leq T
,

\displaystyle\frac{b}{T}\left|\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\langle\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle\langle X_{t},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle-\langle\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{bd_{1}d_{2}}{T}\frac{2}{\varepsilon_{t}}\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|_{\max}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d_{2}}}\|\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\|_{\rm{F}}+\frac{1}{T}\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|_{\max}\|\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{d_{1}d_{2}^{1/2}\sqrt{\mu r}}{T^{1-\gamma}}\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|_{\max}\|\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\|_{\rm{F}}\lesssim\frac{d_{1}d_{2}^{1/2}\sqrt{\mu r}\sigma}{T^{1-\gamma}}\|\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\|_{\rm{F}}.

Moreover,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{b^{2}d_{1}^{2}d_{2}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{T^{2}\pi_{t}^{2}}\langle\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle^{2}\langle X_{t},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\leq\frac{b^{2}d_{1}d_{2}}{T^{2}}\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|_{\max}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{1}{\pi_{t}}\langle X_{t},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{b^{2}d_{1}d_{2}}{T^{2}}\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|_{\max}^{2}\sum_{X\in\mathcal{X}}\mathbf{1}(\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X\rangle>0)\frac{1}{1-\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{2}}\langle X,\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\mathbf{1}(\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X\rangle<0)\frac{1}{\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{2}}\langle X,\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}r\log^{4}d_{1}\sigma^{2}}{T^{3-\gamma}}\sum_{X\in\mathcal{X}}\mathbf{1}(\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X\rangle>0)\frac{1}{1-\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{2}}\langle X,\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\mathbf{1}(\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X\rangle<0)\frac{1}{\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{2}}\langle X,\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}.

Denote
S_{T}^{2}:=\frac{1}{T^{1+\gamma}}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\sum_{X\in\mathcal{X}}\mathbf{1}(\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X\rangle>0)\frac{1}{1-\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{2}}\langle X,\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}+\mathbf{1}(\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{0,t-1},X\rangle<0)\frac{1}{\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{2}}\langle X,\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle^{2}
. By martingale Bernstein inequality, with probability at least
1-d_{1}^{-10}
,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\langle\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle\langle X_{t},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle-\langle\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle\right|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}\log^{5}d_{1}}{T^{2-2\gamma}}}\sigma S_{T}.

As we have shown in the proof of Lemma
4
,
S_{T}\rightarrow S
when
T,d_{1},d_{2}\rightarrow\infty
. Then

\frac{\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\langle\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle\langle X_{t},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle-\langle\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1},\mathcal{P}_{M}(Q)\rangle}{\sigma S\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}/T^{1-\gamma}}}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}0
as long as
\frac{d_{1}\log^{5}d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}\rightarrow 0
.
∎

D.6
Proof of Lemma
6

Proof.

We first state the following Lemmas.

Lemma 9
.

Under the conditions in Theorem
3
, with probability at least
1-d_{1}^{-3}
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{Z}\|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\sigma.

Lemma 10
.

Under the conditions in Theorem
3
, with probability at least
1-5d_{1}^{-2}
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}-LL^{\top}\|_{2,\max}\lesssim\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d_{1}}}

\displaystyle\|\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top}-RR^{\top}\|_{2,\max}\lesssim\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d_{2}}}.

Notice that

\displaystyle|\langle\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}\widehat{Z}\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top}-LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}RR^{\top},Q\rangle|\leq\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}\|\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}\widehat{Z}\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top}-LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}RR^{\top}\|_{\max}.

Now by triangular inequality,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}\widehat{Z}\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top}-LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}RR^{\top}\|_{\max}\leq

\displaystyle\|(\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}-LL^{\top})\widehat{Z}RR^{\top}\|_{\max}+\|LL^{\top}\widehat{Z}(\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top}-RR^{\top})\|_{\max}

\displaystyle+\|(\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}-LL^{\top})\widehat{Z}(\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top}-RR^{\top})\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\widehat{Z}\|\left(\|\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}-LL^{\top}\|_{2,{\max}}\|R\|_{2,{\max}}+\|\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top}-RR^{\top}\|_{2,\max}\|L\|_{2,{\max}}\right)

\displaystyle+\|\widehat{Z}\|\|\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}-LL^{\top}\|_{2,{\max}}\|\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top}-RR^{\top}\|_{2,\max}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{\mu rd_{1}^{3/2}d_{2}^{1/2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}.

Then as long as
\frac{\|Q\|_{\ell 1}}{\sigma S\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}/T^{1-\gamma}}}\cdot\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{\mu rd_{1}^{3/2}d_{2}^{1/2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}=\frac{\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}}{S}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{r^{2}d_{1}^{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\rightarrow 0
, we prove the claimed results.
∎

D.7
Proof of Lemma
7

Proof.

Similarly as the proof of Lemma
6
, we again define events
\mathcal{E}_{0}=\{\|\widehat{Z}\|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\sigma:=\delta\}
,
\mathcal{E}_{1}=\{\|\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}-LL^{\top}\|_{2,\max}\lesssim\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d_{1}}},\|\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top}-RR^{\top}\|_{2,\max}\lesssim\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d_{2}}}\}
. Then by Lemma 5 and Lemma 6 in
Xia and Yuan [2021]
, under the
\mathcal{E}_{0}
and
\mathcal{E}_{1}
,

\displaystyle\big|\sum_{k=2}^{\infty}\langle(\mathcal{S}_{A,k}(\widehat{E})A\Theta\Theta^{\top}+\Theta\Theta^{\top}A\mathcal{S}_{A,k}(\widehat{E})),\widetilde{Q}\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}\frac{\mu r}{\lambda_{\min}\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}}\delta^{2}

\displaystyle=\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{\mu rd_{1}^{3/2}d_{2}^{1/2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}},

and

\displaystyle\big|\langle(\widehat{\Theta}\widehat{\Theta}^{\top}-\Theta\Theta^{\top})A(\widehat{\Theta}\widehat{\Theta}^{\top}-\Theta\Theta^{\top}),\widetilde{Q}\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}\|\Lambda\|\|\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}-LL^{\top}\|_{2,{\max}}\|\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top}-RR^{\top}\|_{2,\max}

\displaystyle\leq\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{\mu r\kappa d_{1}^{3/2}d_{2}^{1/2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}.

Then as long as
\frac{\|Q\|_{\ell_{1}}}{S}\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{r^{2}d_{1}^{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\rightarrow 0
, we prove the claimed results.
∎

D.8
Proof of Lemma
8

Proof.

Without loss of generality, we just prove the
i=1
case and
i=0
case can be shown following the same arguments.

Recall
\delta
defined in Theorem
3
. Suppose
\langle M_{1}-M_{0},X\rangle>0
, then we have for any
T_{0}+1\leq t\leq T
,

\displaystyle\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t}-\widehat{M}_{0,t},X\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t}-M_{1},X\rangle+\langle M_{0}-\widehat{M}_{0,t},X\rangle+\langle M_{1}-M_{0},X\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\langle M_{1}-M_{0},X\rangle-2\delta>0

with probability at least
1-8td_{1}^{-200}
. The second inequality comes from the gap condition in Assumption
1
. As a result, with the same probability,
\mathbbm{1}(\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t}-\widehat{M}_{0,T},X\rangle>0)=\mathbbm{1}(\langle M_{1}-M_{0},X\rangle>0)
.
∎

D.9
Proof of Lemma
9

Proof.

We first look at
\|\widehat{Z}_{1}\|=\|\frac{b}{T}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbf{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\xi_{t}X_{t}\|
.

Since
\xi_{t}
is subGaussian random variable, then for any
T_{0}+1\leq t\leq T
,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\|\frac{b}{T}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbf{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\xi_{t}X_{t}\|\bigg\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq\frac{2bd_{1}d_{2}}{T\varepsilon_{t}}\|\xi_{t}\|_{\psi_{2}}\lesssim\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\sigma}{T^{1-\gamma}}.

Meanwhile,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{b^{2}}{T^{2}}\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}^{2}\mathbf{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\xi_{t}^{2}X_{t}X_{t}^{\top}\bigg|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\leq\frac{b^{2}d_{1}^{2}d_{2}^{2}}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{2}{\varepsilon_{t}}\sigma^{2}{\mathbb{E}}[X_{t}X_{t}^{\top}]

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{b^{2}d_{1}^{2}d_{2}^{2}}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{2}{\varepsilon_{t}}\sigma^{2}\frac{1}{d_{1}}\boldsymbol{I}_{d_{1}}\lesssim\frac{d_{1}d_{2}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{T^{1-\gamma}}\boldsymbol{I}_{d_{1}}.

By symmetry, we can have

\displaystyle\max\left\{\bigg\|\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{b^{2}}{T^{2}}\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}^{2}\mathbf{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\xi_{t}^{2}X_{t}X_{t}^{\top}\bigg|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\bigg\|,\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad\quad\bigg\|\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{b^{2}}{T^{2}}\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}^{2}\mathbf{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\xi_{t}^{2}X_{t}^{\top}X_{t}\bigg|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\bigg\|\right\}\lesssim\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}\sigma^{2}}{T^{1-\gamma}}.

Then by matrix Bernstein inequality, with probability at least
1-d_{1}^{-2}
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{Z}_{1}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\sigma}{T^{1-\gamma}}\log d_{1}+\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}\sigma^{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\sigma.

The first term will be dominated by the second term since
T\gg d_{1}^{1/(1-\gamma)}\log^{3}d_{1}
.

To prove the upper bound for
\|\widehat{Z}_{2}\|=\|\frac{b}{T}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbf{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\langle\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle X_{t}-\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|
, we follow the same arguments by observing that for any
T_{0}+1\leq t\leq T
,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{b}{T}\left(\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbf{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\langle\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle X_{t}-\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\right)\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{bd_{1}d_{2}}{T}\frac{2}{\varepsilon_{t}}\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|_{\max}+\frac{b}{T}\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{d_{1}d_{2}}{T^{1-\gamma}}\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|_{\max}+\frac{\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}}{T}\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|_{\max}\lesssim\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\sigma}{T^{1-\gamma}}.

The second inequality comes from the fact that
\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|\leq\sqrt{d_{1}d_{2}}\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|_{\max}
, and in the last inequality we again use
\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|_{\max}\lesssim\sigma
.

Meanwhile,

\displaystyle\quad\bigg\|\frac{b^{2}}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbf{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\langle\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle X_{t}-\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\right)\left(\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbf{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\langle\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle X_{t}-\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\right)^{\top}\big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{b^{2}}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\bigg\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}^{2}\mathbf{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}^{2}}\langle\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle^{2}X_{t}X_{t}^{\top}\big|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\bigg\|+\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{b^{2}}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{2d_{1}^{2}d_{2}^{2}}{\varepsilon_{t}}\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|_{\max}^{2}\|{\mathbb{E}}(X_{t}X_{t}^{\top})\|+d_{1}d_{2}\|\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}\|_{\max}^{2}\lesssim\frac{d_{1}d_{2}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{T^{1-\gamma}}.

Finally, by matrix Bernstein inequality, with probability
1-d_{1}^{-2}
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{Z}_{2}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\sigma.

∎

D.10
Proof of Lemma
10

Proof.

Consider the event in Theorem
9

{\mathcal{E}}=\bigg\{\|\widetilde{Z}\|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\sigma\bigg\}.

The following proof is adapted from Theorem 4 and Lemma 9 in
Xia and Yuan [2021]
. We adapt it to martingale version.
Under
{\mathcal{E}}
, we have
\|\widehat{Z}\|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\sigma\lesssim\lambda_{\min}=:\delta
.
As long as
\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\sigma\lesssim\lambda_{\min}
, we have
\|\widehat{E}\|=\|\widehat{Z}\|\lesssim\lambda_{\min}
.
Recall the
\mathfrak{P}^{-s}
for
s\geq 0
and
\mathfrak{P}^{\perp}
defined in the proof of Theorem
3
. Following the proof in
Xia and Yuan [2021]
, it suffices to show under
{\mathcal{E}}
, there exist absolute constants
C_{1}
,
C_{2}>0
so that for all
k\geq 0
, the following bounds hold with probability at least
1-2(k+1)d_{1}^{-2}
,

\displaystyle\max_{j\in[d_{1}]}\|e_{j}^{\top}\mathfrak{P}^{\perp}(\mathfrak{P}^{\perp}\widehat{E}\mathfrak{P}^{\perp})^{k}\widehat{E}\Theta\|\leq C_{1}(C_{2}\delta)^{k+1}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d_{1}}},

\displaystyle\max_{j\in[d_{2}]}\|e_{j+d_{1}}^{\top}\mathfrak{P}^{\perp}(\mathfrak{P}^{\perp}\widehat{E}\mathfrak{P}^{\perp})^{k}\widehat{E}\Theta\|\leq C_{1}(C_{2}\delta)^{k+1}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d_{2}}}.

Here we only present the proof for
k=0
and the cases
k\geq 1
can be similarly extended.
Clearly, for
j\in[d_{1}]
,

\displaystyle\|e_{j}^{\top}\mathfrak{P}^{\perp}\widehat{E}\Theta\|\leq\|e_{j}^{\top}\Theta\Theta^{\top}\widehat{E}\Theta\|+\|e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{E}\Theta\|\leq\delta\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d_{1}}}+\|e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{E}\Theta\|=\delta\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d_{1}}}+\|e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{Z}R\|.

We write

\displaystyle e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{Z}R

\displaystyle=\frac{b}{T}\left(\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\xi_{t}e_{j}^{\top}X_{t}R\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\langle\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle e_{j}^{\top}X_{t}R-e_{j}\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}R\right).

Obviously, for any
T_{0}+1\leq t\leq T
,

\displaystyle\left\|\big\|\frac{bd_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{T\pi_{t}}e_{j}^{\top}\xi_{t}X_{t}R\big\|\right\|_{\psi_{2}}\lesssim\frac{d_{1}d_{2}}{T^{1-\gamma}}\sigma\|R\|_{2,\max}\leq\frac{d_{1}d_{2}}{T^{1-\gamma}}\sigma\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d_{2}}}

and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{b^{2}d_{1}^{2}d_{2}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{T^{2}\pi_{t}^{2}}\xi_{t}^{2}e_{j}^{\top}X_{t}RR^{\top}X_{t}^{\top}e_{j}\bigg|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\lesssim\frac{t^{\gamma}\sigma^{2}d_{1}d_{2}}{T^{2}}\text{tr}(RR^{\top})\leq\frac{t^{\gamma}r\sigma^{2}d_{1}d_{2}}{T^{2}}.

Then by martingale Bernstein inequality, with probability at least
1-d_{1}^{-3}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{b}{T}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\xi_{t}e_{j}^{\top}X_{t}R\right\|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{rd_{1}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\sigma.

Similarly, with the same probability,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{b}{T}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\frac{d_{1}d_{2}\mathbbm{1}(a_{t}=1)}{\pi_{t}}\langle\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1},X_{t}\rangle e_{j}^{\top}X_{t}R-e_{j}\widehat{\Delta}_{t-1}R\right\|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{rd_{1}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\sigma.

Then we can conclude that

\displaystyle\|e_{j}^{\top}\widehat{Z}R\|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{rd_{1}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\sigma

with probability at least
1-3d_{1}^{-3}
. Taking union bound,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\max_{j\in[d_{1}]}\|e_{j}^{\top}\mathfrak{P}^{\perp}\widehat{E}\Theta\|\gtrsim\delta\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d_{1}}}\bigg)\leq 3d_{1}^{-2},

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\max_{j\in[d_{2}]}\|e_{j}^{\top}\mathfrak{P}^{\perp}\widehat{E}\Theta\|\gtrsim\delta\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d_{2}}}\bigg)\leq 3d_{1}^{-2}.

Then following the proof of Lemma 4 in
Xia and Yuan [2021]
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\|\widehat{L}\widehat{L}^{\top}-LL^{\top}\|_{2,\max}\gtrsim\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}^{2}d_{2}\log d_{1}}{T^{1-\gamma}}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{d_{1}}}\bigg|{\mathcal{E}}\bigg)\leq 5d_{1}^{-2}\log^{2}d_{1}.

And similarly we can prove the statement for
\|\widehat{R}\widehat{R}^{\top}-RR^{\top}\|_{2,\max}
.

∎

D.11
Proof of Theorem
B.2

Proof.

Without loss of generality, assume at time
t
, arm 1 is optimal, i.e., for any
k\in[K]\backslash\{1\}
,
\langle M_{1}-M_{k},X_{t}\rangle>0
, then the expected regret is

\displaystyle r_{t}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{k\in[K]\backslash\{1\}}\langle M_{1}-M_{k},X_{t}\rangle\mathbbm{1}(\text{choose arm k})\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{k\in[K]\backslash\{1\}}\langle M_{1}-M_{k},X_{t}\rangle\mathbbm{1}(\text{choose arm k},\langle\widehat{M}_{1,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{k,t-1},X_{t}\rangle\geq 0)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{k\in[K]\backslash\{1\}}\langle M_{1}-M_{k},X_{t}\rangle\mathbbm{1}(\text{choose arm 0},\langle\widehat{M}_{k,t-1}-\widehat{M}_{1,t-1},X_{t}\rangle>0)\right]
